### [1] [Infinite-Order Lattice Chiral Anomalies and CPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12510)
*Elijah Lew-Smith,Salvatore D. Pace,Shu-Heng Shao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that the Onsager symmetry—a lattice version of 1+1d chiral symmetry—exhibits an order-two anomaly, but when lattice CPT symmetry is imposed, the anomaly becomes infinite-order, aligning more closely with the continuum chiral anomaly. It also explores associated 2+1d symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases.
\n\n
本文证明了Onsager对称性（一种1+1维手征对称性的格点实现）具有二阶反常，但当施加格点CPT对称性时，该反常被提升为无限阶，从而更忠实地匹配连续时空中的手征反常。文章还探讨了与此类无限阶格点反常对应的2+1维对称性保护拓扑（SPT）相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding how anomalies of global symmetries manifest on the lattice versus in the continuum. While continuum perturbative anomalies (e.g., chiral anomalies captured by triangle Feynman diagrams) are infinite-order, many known lattice anomalies are finite-order. The authors aim to construct a lattice chiral symmetry whose anomaly structure better emulates the infinite-order nature of the continuum chiral anomaly, thereby improving lattice regularization fidelity for chiral theories.
\n\n
动机源于理解全局对称性的反常在格点与连续时空中的不同表现。尽管连续时空中的微扰反常（例如由三角费曼图捕捉的手征反常）是无限阶的，但许多已知的格点反常却是有限阶的。作者旨在构建一种格点手征对称性，其反常结构能更好地模拟连续手征反常的无限阶特性，从而提升对手征理论的格点正则化的保真度。

Method: The authors analyze the Onsager symmetry—an exact lattice symmetry acting as a discrete analog of 1+1d chiral symmetry—and compute its anomaly order by examining the diagonal symmetry of $n$-copy systems. They then impose lattice CPT symmetry and study how this constraint modifies the anomaly classification, using group cohomology and anomaly inflow arguments to show the enhancement from order two to infinite order. The connection to 2+1d SPT phases is established via the bulk-boundary correspondence for symmetry-protected topological order.
\n\n
作者分析了Onsager对称性——一种作为1+1维手征对称性离散类比的精确格点对称性——并通过考察$n$个复制系统的对角对称性来计算其反常阶数。随后，他们施加格点CPT对称性约束，并利用群上同调（group cohomology）和反常流入（anomaly inflow）论证，研究该约束如何改变反常分类，从而证明反常从二阶提升为无限阶。通过SPT序的体边对应关系，建立了与2+1维对称性保护拓扑（SPT）相的联系。

Result: The key results are: (1) the Onsager symmetry alone has an order-two anomaly; (2) imposing lattice CPT symmetry upgrades this to an infinite-order anomaly, matching the structure of the continuum chiral anomaly; and (3) such infinite-order lattice anomalies correspond to nontrivial 2+1d SPT phases protected by the combined Onsager and CPT symmetries.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 单独的Onsager对称性具有二阶反常；(2) 施加格点CPT对称性后，该反常被提升为无限阶，与连续手征反常的结构一致；(3) 此类无限阶格点反常对应于由Onsager与CPT联合对称性保护的非平凡2+1维SPT相。

Conclusion: The work establishes that lattice models can realize infinite-order anomalies—previously thought to be exclusive to the continuum—by incorporating additional symmetries like CPT. This provides a more faithful lattice regularization of chiral field theories and reveals a rich interplay between anomaly order, symmetry constraints, and higher-dimensional topological phases.
\n\n
本工作表明，通过引入CPT等额外对称性，格点模型可以实现原本被认为仅存在于连续时空中的无限阶反常。这为手征场论提供了更忠实的格点正则化方案，并揭示了反常阶数、对称性约束与高维拓扑相之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: A key property of a global symmetry's anomaly is its order: the smallest integer $n$ for which the diagonal symmetry of the $n$-copy system is anomaly-free. While many familiar lattice anomalies have finite order, perturbative anomalies in the continuum$-$those captured by Feynman diagrams$-$have infinite order. In this paper, we show that the Onsager symmetry, a lattice realization of the chiral symmetry of a 1+1d massless Dirac fermion, has an order-two anomaly. However, imposing lattice CPT symmetry enhances this anomaly from order two to infinite order, yielding a lattice chiral symmetry structure that more faithfully matches the continuum chiral anomaly. We also discuss the corresponding 2+1d symmetry-protected topological phases for these infinite-order lattice anomalies.

</details>


### [2] [Localization of Chiral Electromagnetic Waves on Thick Axion Domain Walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12513)
*Nemanja Kaloper*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: We demonstrate that a finite-width axion domain wall in Maxwell theory supports a robust, localized, chiral electromagnetic mode with linear, gapless dispersion due to helicity-dependent coupling from the axion gradient. This chiral surface photon was previously overlooked.
\n\n
我们证明，在Maxwell理论中，有限宽度的axion domain wall由于axion梯度引起的helicity-dependent耦合，支持一种鲁棒的、局域化的手性电磁模，其具有线性无能隙色散关系。这种手性表面光子在以往分析中被忽略了。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous studies of axion electrodynamics with domain walls did not account for the full spectral boundary value problem arising from finite wall thickness and helicity-dependent interactions, potentially missing localized photon modes. The authors aim to systematically investigate whether such configurations support normalizable chiral electromagnetic excitations.
\n\n
以往对含domain wall的axion电动力学研究未充分考虑由有限厚度和helicity-dependent相互作用所导致的完整谱边界值问题，可能遗漏了局域化的光子模。作者旨在系统研究此类构型是否支持可归一化的手性电磁激发。

Method: The authors formulate Maxwell–axion theory as a spectral boundary value problem, treating the axion domain wall profile with finite width. They analyze the resulting wave equations for electromagnetic fields, which include an effective potential proportional to the axion gradient $\\partial_z \\theta(z)$. By solving these equations, they identify bound-state solutions corresponding to one helicity (polarization), while the opposite helicity experiences a repulsive potential and remains unbound.
\n\n
作者将Maxwell–axion理论表述为一个谱边界值问题，采用具有有限宽度的axion domain wall剖面。他们分析包含有效势（正比于axion梯度 $\\partial_z \\theta(z)$）的电磁场波动方程，通过求解这些方程，识别出对应于某一helicity（偏振）的束缚态解，而相反helicity则经历排斥势，无法形成束缚态。

Result: A normalizable, chiral electromagnetic mode with linear, gapless dispersion relation $\\omega = c |k|$ is found to be generically supported by the axion domain wall. This mode is topologically protected in the sense that its existence depends only on the sign of the axion gradient and persists across a wide range of wall profiles and axion masses.
\n\n
研究发现，axion domain wall普遍支持一种可归一化的手性电磁模，其具有线性无能隙色散关系 $\\omega = c |k|$。该模在某种拓扑意义上受保护：其存在仅依赖于axion梯度的符号，并在广泛的wall剖面和axion质量范围内保持稳定。

Conclusion: Axion domain walls generically host a previously unrecognized chiral surface photon arising from helicity-selective binding due to the axion gradient. This finding enriches the understanding of topological responses in axion electrodynamics and may have implications for condensed matter analogs and early-universe cosmology.
\n\n
axion domain wall普遍承载一种此前未被认识到的手性表面光子，它源于axion梯度导致的helicity选择性束缚。这一发现深化了对axion电动力学中拓扑响应的理解，并可能对凝聚态类比系统和早期宇宙cosmology产生影响。

Abstract: We analyze Maxwell theory coupled to an axion domain wall as a spectral boundary value problem. We find that a finite-width axion domain wall generically supports a localized normalizable chiral electromagnetic mode with linear, gapless dispersion. This mode arises from helicity-dependent coupling sourced by the axion gradient: one polarization experiences an effective attractive potential and forms a bound state, while the opposite polarization is repelled. The existence of this chiral surface photon is robust over a wide regime of wall structures and axion masses. Our result shows that axion domain walls generically support a localized chiral photon that has been missed in previous analyses.

</details>


### [3] [Mapping the Infrared Phase Space of Gravity to Finite Subregions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12515)
*Luca Ciambelli,Temple He,Marc S. Klinger,Kathryn M. Zurek*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper establishes a symplectomorphism between the phase space of an arbitrary cut on a null hypersurface in Minkowski spacetime and the infrared (IR) phase space of asymptotically flat gravity, identifying geometric fluctuations of the cut with the leading soft graviton mode and relating the supertranslation Goldstone mode to the product of the cut’s area and its conjugate null time offset.
\n\n
本文建立了闵可夫斯基时空（Minkowski spacetime）中任意零超曲面切片（cut）的相空间与渐近平直引力（asymptotically flat gravity）红外（IR）相空间之间的辛同构（symplectomorphism），将切片的几何涨落对应于领头软引力子模（leading soft graviton mode），并将超平移（supertranslation）戈德斯通模（Goldstone mode）关联到切片面积与其共轭零时间偏移（null time offset）的乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding the infrared structure of quantum gravity in asymptotically flat spacetimes, particularly how symmetries like BMS supertranslations manifest in local geometric data on finite null hypersurfaces. By constructing a finite-region analog of the asymptotic IR phase space, the authors aim to bridge local gravitational physics with asymptotic symmetry principles.
\n\n
该研究的动机源于理解渐近平直时空（asymptotically flat spacetimes）中量子引力的红外结构，特别是BMS超平移（BMS supertranslations）等对称性如何在有限零超曲面上的局域几何数据中体现。通过构建渐近红外相空间的有限区域类比，作者旨在连接局域引力物理与渐近对称性原理。

Method: The authors construct the covariant phase space for an arbitrary cut of a null hypersurface in Minkowski spacetime using the covariant phase space formalism. They then explicitly demonstrate a symplectomorphism—i.e., a canonical transformation preserving the symplectic structure—between this phase space and the known infrared phase space of asymptotically flat gravity at null infinity. Key variables include the fluctuation of the cut (encoding shape deformations) and its symplectic partner, the null time offset. The mapping identifies these with the soft graviton mode and its conjugate variable.
\n\n
作者使用协变相空间形式（covariant phase space formalism）构建了闵可夫斯基时空中零超曲面任意切片的协变相空间。随后，他们明确展示了该相空间与类光无穷远（null infinity）处渐近平直引力已知红外相空间之间存在一个辛同构（即保持辛结构的正则变换）。关键变量包括切片的涨落（编码形状形变）及其辛共轭量——零时间偏移（null time offset）。该映射将这些变量分别对应于软引力子模及其共轭变量。

Result: The main result is the explicit construction of a symplectomorphism between the finite-cut phase space and the asymptotic IR phase space of gravity. Under this map: (1) fluctuations of the cut correspond to the leading soft graviton mode; (2) the supertranslation Goldstone mode is identified with the product of the cut’s “size” (e.g., area or radius) and the null time offset, which serves as its symplectic conjugate. This provides a finite-region realization of asymptotic BMS symmetry data.
\n\n
主要结果是显式构建了有限切片相空间与引力渐近红外相空间之间的辛同构。在此映射下：(1) 切片的涨落对应于领头软引力子模；(2) 超平移戈德斯通模被识别为切片“尺寸”（如面积或半径）与零时间偏移（作为其辛共轭量）的乘积。这为渐近BMS对称性数据提供了一个有限区域的实现。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that the infrared degrees of freedom of asymptotically flat gravity—particularly those tied to BMS supertranslations and soft gravitons—can be fully encoded in the geometry of finite null cuts in Minkowski space. This suggests a deep connection between local null geometry and asymptotic symmetries, potentially offering new insights into the holographic nature of gravity and the memory effect.
\n\n
该工作表明，渐近平直引力的红外自由度——特别是与BMS超平移和软引力子相关的部分——可以完全编码在闵可夫斯基空间中有限零切片的几何中。这揭示了局域零几何与渐近对称性之间的深刻联系，可能为引力的全息性质和记忆效应（memory effect）提供新的见解。

Abstract: We construct the phase space for an arbitrary cut of a null hypersurface in Minkowski spacetime and demonstrate that it is symplectomorphic to the infrared phase space of asymptotically flat gravity. Fluctuations of the cut are mapped to the leading soft graviton mode. Furthermore, the supertranslation Goldstone mode is mapped to the product of the size of the cut with its symplectic partner, the null time offset.

</details>


### [4] [Multi-entropy in heavy local quenches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12526)
*Kosei Fujiki,Kenya Tasuki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper investigates the dynamics of genuine tripartite entanglement entropy after a heavy local quench in 2D holographic CFTs, showing that it is governed by global geodesic winding selection in the bulk and reproduced via heavy-light vacuum block techniques on the boundary, with universal logarithmic time dependence independent of operator dimension.
\n\n
本文研究了二维全息共形场论（CFT）中重局部淬火后真实三部纠缠熵的动力学行为，表明其由体空间中的整体测地线缠绕选择所主导，并可通过边界上的重-轻真空块近似复现，其时间依赖性呈普适的对数形式，且与算符维数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how multipartite entanglement evolves after a localized high-energy (heavy) excitation in holographic CFTs, and whether its dynamics are dictated by local quasiparticle propagation or by nonlocal geometric features such as global saddle-point selection in the dual gravitational description.
\n\n
动机在于理解全息CFT中局域高能（重）激发后多体纠缠的演化机制，并探究其动力学是由局域准粒子传播决定，还是由对偶引力描述中的非局域几何特征（如整体鞍点选择）所主导。

Method: The authors compute the genuine multi-entropy for three adjacent intervals using both holographic methods (perturbative and fully back-reacted geometries involving trivalent geodesic networks in conical defect or BTZ spacetimes) and CFT techniques (heavy-light vacuum block approximation with uniformization maps). They compare bulk winding configurations of geodesics with branch choices in the boundary conformal map.
\n\n
作者通过全息方法（包括锥形缺陷或BTZ时空中的三价测地线网络的微扰及完全反作用几何）和CFT技术（采用重-轻真空块近似及一致化映射）计算三个相邻区间的genuine multi-entropy，并将体空间中测地线的缠绕构型与边界共形映射中的分支选择进行对比。

Result: At first-order perturbation, the genuine multi-entropy vanishes identically at all times. In the fully back-reacted geometry, it becomes nonzero due to a mismatch between the winding of the trivalent geodesic network and that of pairwise geodesics. In the sharp quench limit, the result takes the universal form $ S^{(3)}_{\\text{gen}}(t) \\sim \\log\\left(\\frac{P(t)}{Q(t)}\\right) $, where $ P, Q $ are rational functions of time, independent of the heavy operator dimension $ \\Delta_H $. The CFT calculation within the heavy-light vacuum block reproduces this exact expression.
\n\n
在一阶微扰下，genuine multi-entropy在所有时刻恒为零。在完全反作用几何中，由于三价测地线网络的缠绕与成对测地线各自选择的缠绕不匹配，该量变为非零。在尖锐淬火极限下，结果具有普适形式 $ S^{(3)}_{\\text{gen}}(t) \\sim \\log\\left(\\frac{P(t)}{Q(t)}\\right) $，其中 $ P, Q $ 是时间的有理函数，且与重算符维数 $ \\Delta_H $ 无关。在重-轻真空块近似下的CFT计算精确复现了该表达式。

Conclusion: Genuine tripartite entanglement after a heavy local quench is not governed by local energy transport or quasiparticle spreading, but by global topological/geometric data encoded in the choice of dominant saddle (winding sector) in the holographic bulk. This reflects a non-perturbative, collective feature of quantum gravity duals to heavy states in CFT.
\n\n
重局部淬火后的真三部纠缠并非由局域能量输运或准粒子扩散所主导，而是由全息体空间中主鞍点（缠绕扇区）选择所编码的整体拓扑/几何数据决定。这反映了CFT中重态对应的量子引力对偶所具有的非微扰、集体性质。

Abstract: We study the time evolution of tripartite entanglement in heavy local quenches in two-dimensional holographic conformal field theories. Our diagnostic is the genuine multi-entropy of adjacent intervals, computed from both bulk and boundary perspectives. A perturbative bulk analysis shows that the first-order small-mass perturbation around the vacuum geodesic network cancels identically at any time after the quench. In the fully back-reacted geometry, a vacuum-subtracted genuine multi-entropy arises from a mismatch between the winding selected by the trivalent geodesic network and the windings selected independently by the pairwise geodesics. In the sharp quench limit, the time dependence of genuine multi-entropy is kinematically fixed to logarithms of rational functions of time and is independent of the heavy operator dimension. The CFT calculation reproduces the same formula within the heavy-light vacuum block approximation, where the branch choice in the heavy-background uniformization map corresponds to the winding selection in the bulk. These results indicate that, in this setup, the genuine multi-entropy is controlled by global saddle selection, rather than by a local energy response or quasiparticle propagation.

</details>


### [5] [How traversable is a traversable wormhole?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12528)
*Ben Freivogel,Alessandro Fumagalli,Marija Tomašević*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper investigates low-frequency scattering in the 4D traversable wormhole geometry of Maldacena, Milekhin, and Popov (MMP). It finds that scalar probes exhibit time-dependent transmission with strong initial suppression but eventual leakage, asymptoting to half the black hole absorption cross-section, along with perfect transmission resonances. In contrast, charged massless fermions transmit with near-unit probability at low energies due to a mechanism akin to the Callan-Rubakov effect, making them ideal information carriers, while scalars better reveal the wormhole’s unique structure.
\n\n
本文研究了Maldacena-Milekhin-Popov（MMP）四维可穿越虫洞中的低频散射。研究发现，标量探针表现出时间依赖的透射行为：初始透射被强烈抑制，但随时间逐渐泄漏，最终累积透射截面趋近于对应黑洞吸收截面的一半，并存在完美透射的共振频率。相比之下，带电无质量费米子在低能下以接近单位概率穿越虫洞，其机制类似于Callan-Rubakov效应，使其成为理想的信息载体；而标量探针则更适合揭示此类磁虫洞的独特结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand how different quantum fields—specifically scalars versus charged massless fermions—behave when scattered off or transmitted through the MMP traversable wormhole. This addresses fundamental questions about wormhole traversability, the role of probe type and energy scale, and potential connections to known phenomena like magnetic black hole absorption and the Callan-Rubakov effect in monopole-fermion scattering.
\n\n
动机在于理解不同量子场（特别是标量场与带电无质量费米子）在MMP可穿越虫洞中的散射与透射行为。这涉及关于虫洞可穿越性的基本问题、探针类型与能量尺度的作用，以及与磁黑洞吸收和单极子-费米子散射中的Callan-Rubakov效应等已知现象的潜在联系。

Method: The authors perform a detailed analysis of low-frequency scattering amplitudes in the background of the four-dimensional MMP wormhole solution, which arises in Einstein-Maxwell theory coupled to additional matter fields. They compute transmission probabilities for both neutral/charged scalar fields and charged massless fermions by solving the corresponding wave equations in this geometry. Time-dependent behavior is extracted via wave packet analysis, and late-time asymptotics are compared to black hole absorption cross-sections. Resonances are identified through poles in the transmission amplitude, while the fermionic case is analyzed using techniques analogous to those in magnetic monopole scattering.
\n\n
作者在四维MMP虫洞背景（源于爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论耦合额外物质场）中对低频散射振幅进行了详细分析。他们通过求解相应波动方程，计算了中性/带电标量场和带电无质量费米子的透射概率。利用波包分析提取时间依赖行为，并将晚期渐进行为与黑洞吸收截面进行比较。通过透射振幅的极点识别共振，而费米子情形则采用类似于磁单极子散射的技术进行分析。

Result: For scalar probes (neutral or charged), transmission is parametrically suppressed at early times ($t \\sim$ light-crossing time), with most signal reflected or temporarily trapped; at late times, the accumulated transmission cross-section $\\sigma_{\\text{trans}} \\to \\frac{1}{2} \\sigma_{\\text{abs}}^{\\text{BH}}$. The spectrum also shows discrete resonant frequencies with perfect ($T=1$) transmission. In stark contrast, charged massless fermions exhibit near-unit transmission probability ($T \\approx 1$) at low frequencies due to an angular momentum barrier suppression mechanism linked to their coupling to the wormhole's magnetic field—a phenomenon analogous to the Callan-Rubakov effect in monopole physics.
\n\n
对标量探针（中性或带电），早期时间（$t \\sim$ 光穿越时间）透射被参数化抑制，大部分信号被反射或暂时囚禁；晚期累积透射截面满足 $\\sigma_{\\text{trans}} \\to \\frac{1}{2} \\sigma_{\\text{abs}}^{\\text{BH}}$。透射谱还显示出离散的共振频率，在这些频率处透射率为完美（$T=1$）。相比之下，带电无质量费米子在低频下表现出近单位透射概率（$T \\approx 1$），这是由于其与虫洞磁场耦合导致的角动量势垒抑制机制——这一现象类似于单极子物理中的Callan-Rubakov效应。

Conclusion: The nature of the probe fundamentally determines traversability in magnetic wormholes: scalars reveal structural features through time-delayed leakage and resonances but suffer strong low-frequency suppression, whereas charged massless fermions traverse efficiently due to a topological scattering mechanism. Thus, fermions are optimal for information transfer through such wormholes, while scalars serve as better diagnostic tools for probing their geometry.
\n\n
探针的性质从根本上决定了磁虫洞的可穿越性：标量场通过延迟泄漏和共振揭示结构特征，但在低频下遭受强烈抑制；而带电无质量费米子则因拓扑散射机制而高效穿越。因此，费米子是此类虫洞中信息传递的最佳载体，而标量场则是探测其几何结构的更优诊断工具。

Abstract: To answer the above question, we study low-frequency scattering in the four-dimensional traversable wormhole of Maldacena, Milekhin, and Popov. The resulting transmission probabilities reveal that wormhole traversability depends strongly on the nature of the probe. For scalar probes, both neutral and charged, traversability depends on the time scale. On time scales of order the light-crossing time after sending in a signal, the transmission is parametrically suppressed, with most of the incoming signal reflected or temporarily trapped inside the wormhole throat. As time progresses, the trapped signal gradually leaks out, so that at late times the accumulated transmission cross-section approaches one half of the corresponding black hole absorption cross-section. Despite this generic suppression at low frequencies, the transmission spectrum also exhibits resonant frequencies at which transmission becomes perfect. Charged massless fermions tell a very different story. Unlike scalars, they traverse the wormhole with essentially unit probability at low energies. The same mechanism underlies their efficient absorption by magnetic black holes and realizes a channel closely analogous to the Callan-Rubakov effect, revealing unexpected connections with monopole-fermion scattering. Putting everything together, we conclude that scalar probes are best suited for uncovering distinct features of these magnetic wormholes, while charged massless fermions are the ideal carriers of information through them.

</details>

Abstract: We construct Wigner-like equations of motion for symmetric continuous-spin fields in anti-de Sitter space of arbitrary dimension, treating both the bosonic and fermionic cases. 本文利用非平衡量子场论技术，为不稳定真空QED中的辐射过程建立了一个自洽的实时光微扰框架。它给出了光子产生和平均场的显式背景协变公式，分别达到两圈（$\\alpha^2$）和三顶点（$e^3$）阶，为超越恒定场近似的高精度强场QED预言奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative description of vacuum radiative processes in quantum electrodynamics with a prescribed external electromagnetic background capable of producing electron-positron pairs.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The authors employ a two-field inflationary framework with an inflaton and a light axion spectator. The formalism uses the $\delta N$ formalism and super-horizon effective theory of curvature perturbations sourced by entropy modes.

Result: The mechanism produces a nearly scale-invariant enhancement of the curvature power spectrum, reduces the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$, and shifts the scalar spectral index $n_s$ to a weighted average of adiabatic and entropic tilts at horizon crossing. 与轻轴子旁观场的引力相互作用可以显著改变暴胀后的超视界动力学，为将简单暴胀模型与CMB数据对齐提供了一种稳健且与势能形式无关的途径，同时预言了可观测的非高斯信号。这凸显了即使在等曲率模初始占比较小时，多场引力效应的重要性。

Abstract: We propose a generic, largely inflaton-potential-independent mechanism in which a light axion spectator, initialized near the hilltop of its potential, reshapes inflationary observables through purely gravitational multi-field dynamics. During inflation the axion is frozen and the background follows an effectively single-field trajectory. The same dynamics predict local non-Gaussianity, $f_{\rm NL}^{\rm loc.}\sim \mathcal{O}(1)$, within reach of upcoming surveys.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higher-form gauge theories possess generalized global symmetries (higher-form symmetries) whose charged objects are extended operators like Wilson and 't Hooft loops/surfaces. The authors aim to construct a universal state-defect correspondence grounded in symmetry principles—specifically, the mixed anomaly between electric and magnetic higher-form symmetries—which yields infinitely many conserved charges even without conformal invariance.

高形式规范理论具有广义的整体对称性（高形式对称性），其带电对象是延展算符，如Wilson和't Hooft圈/面。尽管在共形场论（CFT）中态-算符对应已被充分理解，但对于一般的非共形高形式规范理论，此类映射尚属空白。作者旨在基于对称性原理——特别是电与磁高形式对称性之间的混合反常——构建一种普适的态-缺陷对应，该反常即使在无共形不变性的情况下也能产生无穷多守恒荷。

These charges generate an extended Kac-Moody algebra that acts on both the Hilbert space on $S^p \\times S^{d-p-1}$ and on extended defect operators. They construct highest-weight representations of this algebra and demonstrate that energy eigenstates (particularly squeezed states) correspond precisely to Wilson-'t Hooft defects dressed with local gauge-invariant operators. The analysis is then extended to non-linear electrodynamics with interactions, showing the robustness of the symmetry structure.

作者研究$d$维时空中的$p$-形式Maxwell理论，识别出源于电$\\mathbb{Z}$ $(d-p-1)$-形式对称性与磁$\\mathbb{Z}$ $p$-形式对称性之间混合’t Hooft反常所产生的无穷多守恒荷。这些荷生成一个扩展的Kac-Moody代数，同时作用于$S^p \\times S^{d-p-1}$上的希尔伯特空间和延展缺陷算符。他们构造该代数的最高权表示，并证明能量本征态（尤其是压缩态）恰好对应于附着了局域规范不变算符的Wilson-'t Hooft缺陷。随后将分析推广至含相互作用的非线性电动力学，表明该对称性结构具有鲁棒性。

Each highest-weight state maps bijectively to a $p$-dimensional Wilson-'t Hooft defect operator dressed with local operators. Conclusion: The work provides a symmetry-based foundation for a state-defect correspondence in higher-form gauge theories that does not rely on conformal invariance. Abstract: We formulate a one-to-one correspondence between states and defects for higher-form gauge theories in arbitrary dimensions. In particular, Wilson-'t Hooft defects dressed with local gauge-invariant operators are mapped to squeezed energy eigenstates. 作者采用Kummer-Poincaré迭代积分理论，涵盖广义字母表（包括线性分母、分圆字母、二次型及平方根值字母），并通过Mellin变换关联其对应的嵌套求和。他们通过对$x=0$处的展开来定义$\\mu$-延拓，并构建闭式表达式或算法程序进行计算。通过(quasi)shuffle积和Hopf代数结构分析其代数性质，将$\\mu$视为基域的扩张。特别关注$\\mu$-延拓是否仍处于原函数空间内，或是否需要扩展（如平方根情形）。

In contrast, square-root cases necessitate higher transcendental functions. Furthermore, the $\\mu$-extension preserves the Hopf algebra structure induced by the (quasi)shuffle product when $\\mu$ is adjoined to the base field. Explicit algorithms or closed forms are provided for constructing these extensions.
\n\n
本文证明：除涉及平方根值字母表（或含中心二项式系数的嵌套求和）的情形外，所有其他迭代积分类别的$\\mu$-延拓均以$\\mu$的多项式形式保留在原函数空间内；而平方根情形则需引入更高超越函数。此外，当将$\\mu$加入基域后，该$\\mu$-延拓在(quasi)shuffle积下保持原有的Hopf代数结构。文中还提供了构造这些延拓的显式算法或闭式解。

Conclusion: The $\\mu$-extension framework provides a consistent and algebraically robust method to incorporate parameter dependence into the function spaces used for evaluating single-scale Feynman integrals. It clarifies the boundary between function classes that close under $\\mu$-deformation and those requiring transcendental generalization, while preserving essential algebraic structures. This advances the formalism for high-precision perturbative calculations in quantum field theory.
\n\n
$\\mu$-延拓框架为将参数依赖性纳入单尺度费曼积分所用函数空间提供了一种一致且代数稳健的方法。它明确了在$\\mu$-形变下哪些函数类封闭、哪些需超越推广的边界，同时保持关键代数结构。这推动了量子场论中高精度微扰计算的形式化发展。

Abstract: The analytic integration of single-scale Feynman integrals emerging in perturbative calculations in quantum field theories can be performed within special classes of functions, which appear as consecutive generalizations of the polylogarithm in form of Kummer-Poincaré iterative integrals over special alphabets and extensions thereof. These are the polylogarithms, Nielsen integrals, the iterated integrals over linear denominator terms, cyclotomic letters, letters induced by quadratic forms, and square-root valued letters. These integrals are solutions of first-order factorizing differential equations. They are related to specific nested sums via the Mellin transform and their expansions around $x=0$. We construct the $μ$-extensions of these iterated integrals and the associated nested sums. We present closed form solutions or provide algorithms in the case of more involved cases to derive the respective $μ$-extensions and study the algebras of the $μ$-extended function spaces. Except for the case of square-root valued alphabets, the $μ$-extension maps into the same function space polynomially in $μ$. This is also the case for the associated nested sums. For square-root valued alphabets or sums containing central binomials, the $μ$-extension leads to higher transcendental functions. In all other cases the $μ$-extension preserves the Hopf algebra structure implied by the (quasi)shuffle product, by supplementing $μ$ to the ground field.

</details>


### [15] [Analytic structure of the QCD phase diagram in the complex-temperature plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12622)
*Gokce Basar,Vladimir V. Skokov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: The paper investigates the QCD phase diagram by analytically continuing temperature $T$ into the complex plane and identifying Yang-Lee edge singularities that bound the domain of analyticity of thermodynamic observables. These complex-$T$ singularities are studied via universal Ising critical scaling, lattice-QCD data at $\\mu = 0$, and explicit effective models (random-matrix and quark-meson). The leading singularity's location is shown to interpolate between a $\\mu^2$-expansion at small chemical potential and a Puiseux form near the critical point. The analysis reveals that complex-$T$ and complex-$\\mu$ singularities share the same scaling variables, enabling cross-validation of critical-point searches. Lattice data combined with a conformal-Pade method yield a continuum estimate for the nearest complex-$T$ singularity consistent with expectations from chiral dynamics.
\n\n
本文通过将温度 $T$ 解析延拓至复平面，研究 QCD 相图的解析结构，并识别出界定热力学观测量解析域的 Yang-Lee 边缘奇点。这些复温度奇点通过普适的 Ising 临界标度律、$\\mu = 0$ 处的格点 QCD 数据以及显式有效模型（随机矩阵模型和夸克-介子模型）进行研究。主导奇点的位置在小化学势下可展开为 $\\mu^2$ 级数，而在临界点附近则过渡到由 Ising 临界标度决定的 Puiseux 形式。分析表明，复 $T$ 和复 $\\mu$ 奇点轨迹由相同的标度变量和映射系数控制，从而为临界点搜寻提供严格的自洽性检验。结合共形-Padé 方法对格点 QCD 数据的分析给出了最近复温度奇点的连续极限位置，其结果与手征极限转变温度和物理质量下手征磁化率峰值温度之间的预期一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding the analytic structure of QCD thermodynamics is essential for mapping the phase diagram, especially in regions where direct lattice simulations are hindered by the sign problem at finite chemical potential $\\mu$. While singularities in the complex chemical potential plane (Yang-Lee zeros) have been extensively studied, the complementary perspective of treating temperature as a complex variable offers new insights into the radius of convergence of Taylor expansions and the nature of phase transitions. This work aims to unify three approaches—universal critical behavior, first-principles lattice data, and solvable effective models—to constrain the location and nature of the leading complex-temperature singularities and their relation to the QCD critical point.
\n\n
理解 QCD 热力学的解析结构对于描绘相图至关重要，尤其是在有限化学势 $\\mu$ 下因符号问题而难以直接进行格点模拟的区域。尽管复化学势平面上的奇点（Yang-Lee 零点）已被广泛研究，但将温度视为复变量的互补视角为泰勒展开的收敛半径和相变本质提供了新见解。本文旨在统一三种方法——普适临界行为、第一性原理格点数据和可解有效模型——以约束主导复温度奇点的位置与性质，并阐明其与 QCD 临界点的关系。

Method: The authors employ a tripartite methodology: (1) universal scaling theory based on the 3D Ising universality class to describe the Puiseux expansion of the leading Yang-Lee edge singularity near the critical point; (2) explicit computation of complex-$T$ singularities in two effective models—the random-matrix model and the quark-meson model—where analytic continuation is tractable; and (3) a first-principles analysis of continuum-extrapolated lattice-QCD data at $\\mu = 0$ using an iterated conformal-Pade approximant to locate the nearest singularity in the complex $T$ plane. They establish that both complex-$T$ and complex-$\\mu$ singularities are governed by the same scaling variables $r \\sim t/|h|^{1/\\beta\\delta}$ and $\\theta \\sim \\mu^2/|h|^{\\Delta/\\beta\\delta}$, with $t = (T - T_c)/T_c$ and $h$ the symmetry-breaking field.
\n\n
作者采用三重方法：(1) 基于三维 Ising 普适类的标度理论，描述临界点附近主导 Yang-Lee 边缘奇点的 Puiseux 展开；(2) 在两个有效模型（随机矩阵模型和夸克-介子模型）中显式计算复温度奇点，其中解析延拓是可行的；(3) 利用迭代共形-Padé 逼近方法分析 $\\mu = 0$ 处连续极限外推的格点 QCD 数据，以定位复 $T$ 平面中最近的奇点。他们证明复 $T$ 和复 $\\mu$ 奇点均由相同的标度变量控制：$r \\sim t/|h|^{1/\\beta\\delta}$ 和 $\\theta \\sim \\mu^2/|h|^{\\Delta/\\beta\\delta}$，其中 $t = (T - T_c)/T_c$，$h$ 为对称性破缺场。

Result: The paper establishes that the leading complex-temperature Yang-Lee edge singularity moves in the complex $T$ plane as $\\mu$ increases, transitioning from a quadratic dependence ($\\propto \\mu^2$) at small $\\mu$ to a Puiseux series $T_{\\text{sing}}(\\mu) = T_c + c_1 \\mu^{2/\\Delta} + \\cdots$ near the critical point, with $\\Delta = \\beta\\delta$ the gap exponent of the 3D Ising model. In effective models, the full trajectory of this singularity is computed explicitly. Using lattice data at $\\mu = 0$, the authors extract the continuum-limit position of the nearest singularity as $T_{\\text{sing}} = (147.5 \\pm 6.5) - i(14.5 \\pm 3.5)$ MeV, whose real part lies between the chiral-limit transition temperature ($\\sim 132$ MeV) and the physical-mass chiral susceptibility peak ($\\sim 155$ MeV), with a nonzero imaginary part confirming the absence of a true phase transition at physical quark masses.
\n\n
本文确立了主导复温度 Yang-Lee 边缘奇点随 $\\mu$ 增大而在复 $T$ 平面中移动，从小 $\\mu$ 下的二次依赖（$\\propto \\mu^2$）过渡到临界点附近的 Puiseux 级数 $T_{\\text{sing}}(\\mu) = T_c + c_1 \\mu^{2/\\Delta} + \\cdots$，其中 $\\Delta = \\beta\\delta$ 是三维 Ising 模型的能隙指数。在有效模型中，该奇点的完整轨迹被显式计算。利用 $\\mu = 0$ 处的格点数据，作者提取出最近奇点的连续极限位置为 $T_{\\text{sing}} = (147.5 \\pm 6.5) - i(14.5 \\pm 3.5)$ MeV，其实部位于手征极限转变温度（$\\sim 132$ MeV）与物理质量下手征磁化率峰值（$\\sim 155$ MeV）之间，非零虚部证实了在物理夸克质量下不存在真实相变。

Conclusion: The complex-temperature perspective provides a powerful and complementary framework to the traditional complex-chemical-potential approach for probing QCD criticality. By demonstrating that complex-$T$ and complex-$\\mu$ singularities stem from the same underlying scaling structure, the work establishes a robust consistency condition for locating the QCD critical point. The extracted singularity from lattice data supports the scenario of a smooth crossover at physical quark masses, and the methodology opens avenues for improved convergence estimates of Taylor expansions and better constraints on the critical scaling window in future lattice and effective-theory studies.
\n\n
复温度视角为探测 QCD 临界性提供了强大且互补于传统复化学势方法的框架。通过证明复 $T$ 与复 $\\mu$ 奇点源于相同的底层标度结构，本工作为定位 QCD 临界点建立了稳健的自洽性条件。从格点数据中提取的奇点支持物理夸克质量下为平滑交叉（crossover）的图像，该方法为改进泰勒展开的收敛性估计、并在未来的格点与有效理论研究中更好地约束临界标度窗口开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We study the analytic structure of the QCD phase diagram by treating temperature as a complex variable. The nearest Yang-Lee edge singularities in the complex $T$ plane bound the domain of analyticity of temperature-dependent thermodynamic observables and complement the more commonly studied singularities in the complex chemical-potential plane. Our analysis combines three complementary perspectives: universal critical scaling, a first-principles extraction from lattice-QCD data, and explicit illustrations in effective models. We illustrate the resulting structure in a random-matrix model and in a quark-meson model, where the singularity trajectories can be followed explicitly. At small real chemical potential, the leading complex-temperature singularity admits an analytic expansion in $μ^2$, while near a critical point it crosses over to the universal Puiseux form dictated by Ising critical scaling. We show that the complex-$T$ and complex-$μ$ trajectories are controlled by the same scaling variables and mapping coefficients, so their comparison provides a stringent consistency test of critical-point searches and constrains the extent of the critical scaling regime. Finally, we analyze lattice-QCD data at $μ=0$ using an iterated conformal-Pade approach and extract the continuum location of the nearest complex-temperature singularity. The result is consistent with the expectation that, at physical quark masses, the real part of the leading singularity lies between the chiral-limit transition temperature and the physical-mass chiral-susceptibility peak temperature, while its imaginary part remains nonzero.

</details>


### [16] [Quantum Stochastic Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12636)
*Robson Christie,Jaewoo Joo,Greg Kaplanek,Vincent Vennin,David Wands*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper reformulates stochastic inflation using the open quantum systems framework, identifying the coarse-grained field and its conjugate momentum as a bulk quantum mode. The continual inclusion of new comoving modes leads to entanglement and tracing over environmental degrees of freedom, resulting in non-unitary GKLS dynamics. This yields a Fokker-Planck equation matching classical stochastic inflation for light fields, while heavy fields remain quantum and resist classical stochastic description.

本文在开放量子系统框架下重新表述了随机暴胀，将粗粒化场及其共轭动量识别为一个“体”（bulk）量子模。新共动模的持续加入导致纠缠，并通过对环境自由度求迹，产生非幺正的GKLS动力学。这导出了与经典随机暴胀一致的Fokker-Planck方程（适用于轻场），而重场则保持量子性，难以用经典随机方法描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to provide a rigorous quantum mechanical foundation for stochastic inflation by treating the coarse-grained super-Hubble field as an open quantum system interacting with sub-Hubble environmental modes. Traditional derivations often assume classical noise; this work aims to derive diffusion and Hubble friction from first-principles quantum dynamics, clarifying when and why a classical stochastic description emerges.
\n\n
动机是通过将超哈勃尺度的粗粒化场视为与亚哈勃环境模相互作用的开放量子系统，为随机暴胀提供严格的量子力学基础。传统推导通常假设经典噪声；本文旨在从第一性原理的量子动力学出发，推导出扩散与哈勃摩擦，并阐明经典随机描述在何时以及为何会出现。

Method: The authors model the coarse-grained field $\\phi$ and its total momentum $\\pi$ in a fixed physical patch as a canonical pair acting on a one-mode Fock space ("bulk"). As time evolves, new comoving modes cross the coarse-graining scale and become part of the bulk, necessitating a redefinition that entangles the old bulk with a new environmental mode. Tracing out this mode yields a Gorini–Kossakowski–Sudarshan–Lindblad (GKLS) master equation: 
$$
\\dot{\\rho} = -i[H_{\\text{eff}}, \\rho] + \\mathcal{D}[L]\\rho,
$$
with an effective Hamiltonian $H_{\\text{eff}}$ and a single Lindblad operator $L$. They perform a Wigner-Weyl transform to obtain a Fokker-Planck equation for the Wigner function and explore quantum unravellings via stochastic Schrödinger equations under continuous measurement. In the light-field limit ($m \\ll H$), they integrate out momentum to recover Starobinsky’s slow-roll Fokker-Planck equation.
\n\n
作者将固定物理尺度斑块中的粗粒化场 $\\phi$ 及其总动量 $\\pi$ 建模为作用于单模Fock空间（“体”）上的正则对。随着时间演化，新的共动模穿越粗粒化尺度并成为“体”的一部分，这一重新定义使旧“体”与新的环境模纠缠。对环境模求迹后得到Gorini–Kossakowski–Sudarshan–Lindblad（GKLS）主方程：
$$
\\dot{\\rho} = -i[H_{\\text{eff}}, \\rho] + \\mathcal{D}[L]\\rho,
$$
其中包含有效哈密顿量 $H_{\\text{eff}}$ 和单一Lindblad算符 $L$。他们通过Wigner-Weyl变换得到Wigner函数的Fokker-Planck方程，并在连续测量下探讨了该GKLS动力学的量子展开（unravelling）——即随机薛定谔方程。在轻场极限（$m \\ll H$）下，通过对动量积分，恢复了Starobinsky的慢滚Fokker-Planck方程。

Result: For a free scalar field in de Sitter, the open quantum system approach yields exact GKLS dynamics where both diffusion and Hubble friction arise from a single quantum channel (one Lindblad operator). The Wigner function obeys a Fokker-Planck equation identical to that of classical stochastic inflation. In the light-field regime ($m \\ll H$), integrating out momentum gives Starobinsky’s equation, and the patch’s purity decays rapidly, indicating strong decoherence. For heavy fields ($m \\gtrsim H$), diffusion is suppressed, the system remains near-pure, and no valid classical stochastic description exists.
\n\n
对于de Sitter时空中的自由标量场，开放量子系统方法导出了精确的GKLS动力学，其中扩散与哈勃摩擦均源于单一量子通道（一个Lindblad算符）。Wigner函数满足的Fokker-Planck方程与经典随机暴胀完全一致。在轻场区域（$m \\ll H$），对动量积分可得Starobinsky方程，且斑块的纯度迅速衰减，表明强退相干。对于重场（$m \\gtrsim H$），扩散被抑制，系统保持近纯态，无法建立有效的经典随机描述。

Conclusion: Stochastic inflation emerges naturally from unitary quantum field theory when the super-Hubble sector is treated as an open quantum system. Classical stochastic behavior is not universal but only arises for light fields due to environmental decoherence. The unified origin of diffusion and friction in a single Lindblad channel provides a deeper quantum understanding of stochastic inflation, bridging quantum foundations and effective classical descriptions.
\n\n
当超哈勃区域被视为开放量子系统时，随机暴胀自然地从幺正量子场论中涌现。经典随机行为并非普适，仅在轻场因环境退相干而出现。扩散与摩擦源于单一Lindblad通道，这一统一机制为随机暴胀提供了更深层的量子理解，架起了量子基础与有效经典描述之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We formulate stochastic inflation in an open quantum system framework. The field coarse-grained in a patch of fixed physical size, and the total momentum of that patch, form a canonical pair and act on a one-mode Fock space which we identify as the "bulk". At each time step, new comoving modes join the coarse-grained patch and the bulk has to be redefined. This redefinition produces an entangled mode that is traced over, yielding a non-unitary evolution equation for the bulk's density matrix. For a free test field in de Sitter, one obtains GKLS dynamics, generated by an effective Hamiltonian and a single non-Hermitian Lindblad operator, hence diffusion and Hubble friction originate from the same quantum channel. The Wigner-Weyl transform of the GKLS equation leads to a Fokker-Planck equation for the Wigner function, which matches the one that applies to the classical phase-space distribution of stochastic inflation. We also provide several schemes under which one can unravel the GKLS dynamics into stochastic Schrodinger equations when continuous measurements of the decoupled mode are performed, making contact with Langevin formulations of stochastic inflation. In the light-field regime, an additional overdamped reduction can be performed by integrating out the momentum variable in the Wigner distribution, leading to Starobinsky's slow-roll Fokker-Planck equation. In that regime, the purity of the patch is strongly suppressed. In contrast, for heavy fields, field diffusion is suppressed and the coarse-grained patch remains close to a pure underdamped oscillator, which prevents a classical stochastic treatment.

</details>

动机在于探究引力场是否必须具备量子特性（特别是非对易算符）才能在远距离质量之间产生纠缠，从而在一个受控的理论模型中为引力的量子本质提供一种操作性检验。

Result: Entanglement between the two particles arises solely from the quantum (non-commuting) part of the gravitational field, as captured by its commutation relations. The onset of entanglement exhibits a time delay $\\Delta t \\sim r/c$, where $r$ is the separation, confirming causal propagation. The magnitude of entanglement is extremely small but can be amplified by preparing the particles in initial squeezed states.
\n\n
两粒子间的纠缠仅源于引力场的量子部分（即非对易部分），由其对易关系所刻画。纠缠的出现存在时间延迟 $\\Delta t \\sim r/c$（$r$ 为间距），证实了相互作用的因果传播性质。纠缠量级极小，但若将粒子制备于初始压缩态，可实现一定程度的增强。

Conclusion: The work supports the view that gravity must be quantized to mediate entanglement between distant masses, as classical gravitational fields cannot generate such correlations. The causal time delay and dependence on field commutators provide key signatures of the quantum gravitational interaction, even if the effect is experimentally challenging to detect.

本工作支持如下观点：若要使远距离质量间产生纠缠，引力必须被量子化，因为经典引力场无法生成此类关联。因果时间延迟及对场对易子的依赖构成了量子引力相互作用的关键特征，尽管其实验探测极具挑战性。

Abstract: In this work, we show how the interaction between propagating modes of the quantized gravitational field and two massive particles trapped in a harmonic oscillator potential can cause the two particles to become entangled. To demonstrate this, we employ an operator-based approach within the framework of the Feynman-Vernon influence functional. Through this method, we find that the effect of the gravitational field on the generated entanglement is encoded in the commutation relations of the gravitational field. This result indicates that, within the framework of the model considered, entanglement arises through the quantum contributions of the gravitational field. Furthermore, this work also shows that entanglement is not formed instantaneously after the two particles interact with the gravitational field. Instead, there exists a time delay, proportional to the distance between the particles, before entanglement is established. This result reflects the causal propagation nature of gravitational interactions. In general, the entanglement generated through this mechanism is extremely small. Nevertheless, if the initial quantum states of the two massive particles are chosen to be squeezed states, the amount of generated entanglement can be enhanced, although the resulting effect remains very small.

</details>


### [18] [Double copy of form factors with multiple operator insertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12929)

TL;DR: The paper introduces a "dyeing" procedure to generalize the double copy to form factors (FFs) with multiple local gauge-invariant operator insertions. 本文引入一种“染色”（dyeing）方法，将双拷贝（double copy）推广到含多个局域规范不变算符插入的形因子（form factors, FFs）。通过将单态算符提升为伴随表示的有色态，FF中的虚假极点被重新解释为染色态的物理传播子，而隐藏的因子化结构则源于染色振幅的BCJ关系。该框架揭示了一种新的标量排序结构，该结构在双拷贝至引力理论中得以保留。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Extending the double copy of scattering amplitudes to more general physical quantities involving local gauge-invariant operators is a central open question. Abstract: Can a classically relevant stress-tensor deformation be exactly solvable, duality preserving, and physically causal? Abstract: We analyze the flow of the background graviton propagator driven by the running of asymptotically safe form factors in four-dimensional quantum gravity at quadratic order in the curvature expansion. Abstract: We study Yang-Mills theory coupled to a particle on a cylinder, where gauge invariance and compactness reduce the dynamics to a finite dimensional quantum system. Abstract: When matter fields are integrated out in a large N approximation, the conformal anomaly induces an effective action up to conformally invariant correction terms. A new one-parameter family of quantum hair emerges which changes the stress tensor in the near-horizon region. This suggests a new semiclassical mechanism by which information about black hole formation could be encoded outside the horizon.

Method: The authors work at a special point in the parameter space of three-dimensional massive gravity where the theory admits a unique maximally symmetric vacuum and supports static extremal hairy black holes. 作者在三维 massive gravity 参数空间的一个特殊点开展工作，该点处理论具有唯一的最大对称真空，并支持静态极端 hairy 黑洞。他们聚焦于近极端黑洞的近视界几何 AdS$_2\times S^1$，通过对在严格极端极限下成为精确零模的边界引力子涨落进行量子化，计算一圈配分函数。熵的对数修正是从该背景上二次涨落算符的行列式中提取的。

Abstract: We study the one-loop correction to the entropy of near-extremal black holes in three-dimensional massive gravity at the special point where the theory exhibits a unique maximally symmetric vacuum and non-constant curvature hairy black holes can achieve extremality even in the static case. Abstract: We construct a graphical coaction for Friedmann-Robertson-Walker (FRW) integrals at all loop orders in conformally-coupled scalar theories with non-conformal polynomial interactions. Abstract: The one-loop five-point function of stress tensor multiplets in N=4 super Yang-Mills theory in four dimensions has previously been studied by integrability methods. For the first time, we present a full analytic evaluation of all processes. The starting point is the re-summation of series of residues into Euler integrals. These are directly integrated where possible or otherwise recovered from intersection theory for generalised hypergeometric functions.

Abstract: The real scalar singlet extension of the standard model provides a minimal framework in which the Higgs sector can realise a strong first-order electroweak phase transition and improve the stability of the electroweak vacuum.   <summary>Details</summary>

Deviations from the SHM reach $\sim80\%$ near the signal maximum. Abstract: The Large Magellanic Cloud (LMC), the most massive satellite of the Milky Way (MW), can significantly perturb the local dark matter (DM) distribution. We study its impact on directional DM detection using the Auriga cosmological simulations of a MW analogue hosting an LMC analogue. We find that the LMC induces strong anisotropies in directional recoil signals, driven primarily by the non-zero mean azimuthal velocity of the local DM distribution. Abstract: The Gertsenshtein effect can be used to probe the stochastic gravitational wave background at high frequencies, well above the range of standard cosmological sources. In this paper, we revisit the conversion between electromagnetic and gravitational waves in the magnetosphere of magnetars by solving the evolution equations of the associated Stokes parameters. In the process, we point out that the adiabatic approximation usually taken in the literature is not generally justified in the context of the Gertsenshtein effect. Result: For gaugino-to-VEV ratios $M_{\tildeλ}/v_X \simeq 0.05$–$0.23$, the model predicts gravitational wave signals with $Ω_{\rm GW}h^2 \sim 3 \times 10^{-10}$ near the percolation boundary, within reach of Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer. The amplitude strongly depends on the portal coupling $δ$: for a cold initial hidden sector, $Ω_{\rm GW}h^2$ rises from the ET noise floor at $δ = 10^{-6}$ to $\sim 7 \times 10^{-11}$ at $δ = 10^{-4}$; a hotter initial state yields large signals even for weak $δ$. Simultaneously, the model achieves the observed dark matter relic density $Ω_{\rm CDM}h^2 = 0.12$ with dark quark masses $m_q \simeq 30$–$800\;$keV and negligible contribution to $N_{\rm eff}$ ($\lesssim {\rm few} \times 10^{-5}$).
\n\n
对于gaugino质量与VEV之比 $M_{\tildeλ}/v_X \simeq 0.05$–$0.23$，该模型预测在逾渗边界附近产生 $Ω_{\rm GW}h^2 \sim 3 \times 10^{-10}$ 的引力波信号，处于Einstein Telescope和Cosmic Explorer的探测范围内。信号振幅强烈依赖于门户耦合 $δ$：若隐藏扇区初始较冷，$Ω_{\rm GW}h^2$ 从 $δ = 10^{-6}$ 时的ET噪声底限上升至 $δ = 10^{-4}$ 时的 $\sim 7 \times 10^{-11}$；若初始较热，则即使在弱 $δ$ 下也能产生强信号。同时，该模型通过暗夸克质量 $m_q \simeq 30$–$800\;$keV 实现观测到的暗物质剩余密度 $Ω_{\rm CDM}h^2 = 0.12$，并对 $N_{\rm eff}$ 的贡献可忽略（$\lesssim {\rm few} \times 10^{-5}$）。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that a minimal supersymmetric hidden sector with a spontaneously broken $U(1)_X$ can simultaneously generate observable gravitational wave signals in next-generation detectors and account for the full dark matter relic abundance through a multi-stage mechanism involving relativistic freeze-out and entropy dilution. The interplay between portal coupling, initial hidden-sector temperature, and soft SUSY-breaking parameters critically shapes both the GW spectrum and dark matter yield, offering testable predictions for future cosmological and collider experiments.
\n\n
研究表明，一个具有自发破缺 $U(1)_X$ 的极小超对称隐藏扇区能够同时在下一代探测器中产生可观测的引力波信号，并通过包含相对论性冻结和熵稀释的多阶段机制解释全部暗物质剩余丰度。门户耦合、隐藏扇区初始温度与软SUSY破缺参数之间的相互作用关键地塑造了引力波谱和暗物质产额，为未来的宇宙学和对撞机实验提供了可检验的预言。

Abstract: We study supercooled first-order phase transitions in a supersymmetric hidden sector with a spontaneously broken $U(1)_X$, focusing on the frequency range of the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer. Along the D-flat direction the tree-level quartic vanishes, so the barrier is generated radiatively by soft SUSY-breaking splittings. In the $\overline{\rm DR}$ scheme the gaugino mass $M_{\tildeλ}$ sets the barrier depth, while the soft scalar mass $m_0$ stabilizes the broken vacuum. For $M_{\tildeλ}/v_X\simeq0.05$--$0.23$, the predicted signal reaches $Ω_{\rm GW}h^2\sim3\times10^{-10}$ near the percolation boundary. The observable amplitude depends sensitively on the portal coupling $δ$ through the hidden-to-visible temperature ratio at percolation: for a cold initial hidden sector the signal rises from the ET floor at $δ=10^{-6}$ to $Ω_{\rm GW}h^2\simeq7\times10^{-11}$ as the sectors approach thermal contact at $δ=10^{-4}$, while a hotter initial hidden sector gives a large signal already for weak portal coupling. We follow this evolution with an 11-variable Boltzmann system that separates the cold nucleating exterior from the reheated true-vacuum interior; reheating mainly enters through the energy budget and redshift factors. The same hidden sector can reproduce $Ω_{\rm CDM}h^2=0.12$ through relativistic dark-quark freeze-out followed by entropy dilution from hidden-Higgs decay, with $m_q\simeq30$--$800\;$keV and $N_{\rm eff}\lesssim{\rm few}\times10^{-5}$.

### [51] [An approximate application of quantum gravity to the rotation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12461)
*R. Michael Jones*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper addresses the "Rotation Problem"—why the observed relative rotation between matter and inertial frames in the universe is extremely small—by showing that quantum cosmology, via a path integral over cosmologies with different root-mean-square (rms) relative rotation rates, suppresses large rotations through phase interference. Using the Einstein-Hilbert action and assuming 50–60 e-foldings of inflation, it finds that only cosmologies with rms rotation rates below $10^{-51}$ rad/year (or $10^{-32}$ rad/year without inflation) contribute significantly to the path integral near $t \\sim 0.25$ s after the Big Bang. The result depends only on fundamental constants and is robust across quantum gravity models.
\n\n
本文探讨了“旋转问题”——即为何观测到的物质与惯性系之间的相对旋转极其微小——并通过量子宇宙学路径积分方法给出解答：在对具有不同均方根（rms）相对旋转率的宇宙学历史进行路径积分时，相位干涉会强烈抑制大旋转。基于Einstein-Hilbert作用量并假设暴胀期间有50–60个e-foldings，研究发现，在大爆炸后约0.25秒时，只有rms旋转率低于$10^{-51}$ rad/年的宇宙（若无暴胀则低于$10^{-32}$ rad/年）对路径积分有显著贡献。该结果仅依赖于基本常数，对量子引力理论的具体细节不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the "Rotation Problem" in General Relativity: while Einstein's equations permit solutions with arbitrarily large relative rotation between matter and local inertial frames (i.e., frame-dragging effects integrated over cosmic scales), observations constrain this relative rotation to be vanishingly small. Classical GR offers no mechanism to explain this fine-tuning. The paper seeks a natural explanation within quantum gravity, specifically using the path integral formulation of quantum cosmology, where interference between histories can dynamically suppress undesirable configurations.
\n\n
动机源于广义相对论中的“旋转问题”：尽管爱因斯坦场方程允许物质分布与局部惯性系之间存在任意大的相对旋转（即在宇宙尺度上累积的参考系拖曳效应），但观测表明这种相对旋转极其微小。经典广义相对论无法解释这种精细调节。本文旨在通过量子引力框架——特别是量子宇宙学的路径积分表述——寻找自然解释，其中不同宇宙历史之间的相位干涉可动态抑制非物理的大旋转构型。

Method: The method employs a semi-classical path integral approach in quantum cosmology, where each "path" corresponds to a distinct Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW)-like cosmology endowed with a non-zero root-mean-square (rms) relative rotation rate $\\omega_{\\text{rms}}$ between matter and inertial frames. The Einstein-Hilbert action is evaluated for these rotating cosmologies. The key step is computing the phase $S/\\hbar$ of each path, where $S$ is the gravitational action. Constructive interference—and thus significant contribution to the path integral—occurs only when the action is stationary ($\\delta S = 0$). The authors show that $S[\\omega_{\\text{rms}}]$ is extremized at $\\omega_{\\text{rms}} = 0$, and for non-zero $\\omega_{\\text{rms}}$, rapid oscillations in $e^{iS/\\hbar}$ lead to destructive interference. The magnitude of suppression depends on dimensionless ratios involving the Hubble parameter $H$, the size of the observable universe $R \\sim c/H$, the Planck time $t_{\\text{Pl}}$, and the number of e-foldings $N_e$ during inflation.
\n\n
方法采用量子宇宙学中的半经典路径积分方法，其中每条“路径”对应一个类FLRW宇宙学模型，但带有非零的物质与惯性系之间的均方根（rms）相对旋转率$\\omega_{\\text{rms}}$。作者计算这些旋转宇宙的Einstein-Hilbert作用量，并分析路径积分中的相位因子$e^{iS/\\hbar}$。关键在于：只有当作用量$S$取极值（$\\delta S = 0$）时，路径才会相干叠加并对路径积分有显著贡献。作者证明$S[\\omega_{\\text{rms}}]$在$\\omega_{\\text{rms}} = 0$处取极值；对于非零$\\omega_{\\text{rms}}$，由于$S/\\hbar$极大，相位快速振荡导致相消干涉。抑制程度由无量纲组合决定，包括哈勃参数$H$、可观测宇宙尺度$R \\sim c/H$、普朗克时间$t_{\\text{Pl}}$以及暴胀期间的e-foldings数$N_e$。

Result: The calculation yields stringent upper bounds on the root-mean-square relative rotation rate $\\omega_{\\text{rms}}$ at $t \\approx 0.25$ seconds after the initial singularity. With standard inflationary scenarios (50–60 e-foldings), $\\omega_{\\text{rms}} \\lesssim 10^{-51}$ rad/year. Even in the absence of inflation, quantum interference still enforces $\\omega_{\\text{rms}} \\lesssim 10^{-32}$ rad/year. These limits arise because the gravitational action for rotating cosmologies scales as $S \\sim (R^2 / t_{\\text{Pl}}^2) \\cdot (\\omega_{\\text{rms}} / H)^2$, making $S/\\hbar \\gg 1$ for any observationally allowed but theoretically permitted large $\\omega_{\\text{rms}}$, thereby suppressing such paths. The extremum of the action occurs precisely at zero rotation, explaining the observed isotropy of inertial frames.
\n\n
计算得出在初始奇点后约0.25秒时，均方根相对旋转率$\\omega_{\\text{rms}}$的严格上限：在标准暴胀情景下（50–60个e-foldings），$\\omega_{\\text{rms}} \\lesssim 10^{-51}$ rad/年；即使没有暴胀，量子干涉仍强制$\\omega_{\\text{rms}} \\lesssim 10^{-32}$ rad/年。这是因为旋转宇宙的引力作用量满足$S \\sim (R^2 / t_{\\text{Pl}}^2) \\cdot (\\omega_{\\text{rms}} / H)^2$，导致对于任何理论上允许但观测上不可见的大$\\omega_{\\text{rms}}$，均有$S/\\hbar \\gg 1$，从而强烈压制此类路径。作用量在零旋转处取极值，自然解释了惯性系观测上的各向同性。

Conclusion: The Rotation Problem is naturally resolved in quantum cosmology without fine-tuning: the path integral over cosmological histories inherently suppresses universes with large relative rotation between matter and inertial frames due to destructive quantum interference. The mechanism is robust—it relies only on the Einstein-Hilbert action, basic cosmological parameters (Hubble scale, size of the visible universe), and the number of inflationary e-foldings—and is insensitive to the ultraviolet completion of quantum gravity. Thus, the observed near-perfect alignment of matter and inertial frames emerges as a generic prediction of quantum gravitational dynamics in the early universe.
\n\n
“旋转问题”在量子宇宙学中无需精细调节即可自然解决：对宇宙学历史的路径积分会因量子相消干涉而内在地压制物质与惯性系之间存在大相对旋转的宇宙。该机制具有鲁棒性——仅依赖于Einstein-Hilbert作用量、基本宇宙学参数（哈勃尺度、可观测宇宙大小）及暴胀e-foldings数——且对量子引力的紫外完备形式不敏感。因此，物质与惯性系观测上的近乎完美对齐，成为早期宇宙量子引力动力学的一个普适预言。

Abstract: Arbitrary initial conditions allow solutions of Einstein's field equations for General Relativity to have arbitrarily large relative rotation of matter and inertial frames. The ``Rotation Problem'' is to explain why the measured relative rotation rate is so small. Nearly any reasonable theory of quantum gravity can solve the rotation problem by phase interference. Even as early as about a quarter of a second after the initial singularity, quantum cosmology would limit the cosmologies that contribute significantly to a path integral calculation to have relative rms rotation rates less than about $10^{-51}$ rad/year. Those calculations are based on using 50 e-foldings during inflation. For 55 or 60 e-foldings, the cosmologies contributing significantly to the path integral would have even smaller relative rotation rates. In addition, although inflation dominates the calculation, even if there had been no inflation, the cosmologies contributing significantly to the path integral would have relative rotation rates less than about $10^{-32}$ rad/year at about a quarter of a second after the initial singularity. These calculations are insensitive to the details of the theory of quantum gravity because the main factor depends only on the size of the visible universe, the Planck time, the free-space speed of light, the Hubble parameter, and the number of e-foldings during inflation. These calculations use the Einstein-Hilbert action in quantum gravity, including large-scale relative rotation of inertial frames and the matter distribution, in which each ``path'' is a cosmology with a different rms relative rotation rate. The calculation shows that the action is an extremum at zero rms relative rotation rate.

### [52] [Black Hole Radiation Sparsity and Bekenstein Entropy Loss in Non-Commutative Schwarzschild Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12470)
*Abdellah Touati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies Hawking radiation properties of a Schwarzschild black hole (SBH) in non-commutative (NC) gravity, showing that NC effects remove temperature divergences, introduce logarithmic entropy corrections, reduce total emitted particles consistent with non-thermal spectra, and lead to extremely sparse radiation ($\hat{\eta} \gg 1$) that diverges as evaporation ends.
\n\n
本文在非对易（NC）引力框架下研究了Schwarzschild黑洞（SBH）的Hawking辐射特性，表明NC效应消除了温度发散，引入了对熵的对数修正，使总辐射粒子数与非热谱一致，并导致辐射极度稀疏（$\hat{\eta} \gg 1$），且在蒸发末期发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the singularities and divergences in classical black hole thermodynamics—particularly the infinite Hawking temperature at zero mass—and the need to incorporate quantum gravitational effects. Non-commutative geometry offers a UV-regularized framework where spacetime coordinates no longer commute, potentially resolving these pathologies while modifying black hole entropy, radiation spectrum, and evaporation dynamics.
\n\n
动机源于经典黑洞热力学中的奇点和发散问题——尤其是质量趋于零时Hawking温度的无穷大——以及引入量子引力效应的必要性。非对易几何提供了一个紫外（UV）正则化的框架，在其中时空坐标不再对易，有望解决这些病态行为，同时修正黑洞熵、辐射谱和蒸发动力学。

Method: The authors employ a non-commutative (NC) gauge theory of gravity to model a Schwarzschild black hole with a smeared mass distribution, replacing the point-like singularity. They compute the deformed Hawking temperature $T_H$ and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy $S$ using this NC metric, derive the Bekenstein entropy loss $\Delta S$, calculate the total emitted particle number $N = \int_0^\infty \frac{dN}{dt} dt$, and evaluate the sparsity parameter $\hat{\eta} = \tau_{\text{gap}} / \tau_{\text{emission}}$, where $\tau_{\text{gap}}$ is the average time between emissions and $\tau_{\text{emission}}$ is the emission duration.
\n\n
作者采用非对易（NC）引力规范理论，通过弥散的质量分布建模Schwarzschild黑洞，以取代点状奇点。他们利用该NC度规计算修正后的Hawking温度$T_H$和Bekenstein-Hawking熵$S$，推导Bekenstein熵损失$\Delta S$，计算总辐射粒子数$N = \int_0^\infty \frac{dN}{dt} dt$，并评估稀疏性参数$\hat{\eta} = \tau_{\text{gap}} / \tau_{\text{emission}}$，其中$\tau_{\text{gap}}$为辐射事件间的平均时间间隔，$\tau_{\text{emission}}$为单次辐射持续时间。

Result: The NC framework yields a finite Hawking temperature that vanishes as the black hole mass approaches the minimal remnant mass, eliminating the usual divergence. The entropy acquires a logarithmic correction: $S = \frac{A}{4G} + \alpha \ln\left(\frac{A}{4G}\right) + \cdots$. The total emitted particle number scales with the initial entropy, confirming non-thermal character. Crucially, the sparsity parameter satisfies $\hat{\eta} \gg 1$ throughout evaporation and diverges ($\hat{\eta} \to \infty$) near the endpoint, indicating radiation becomes increasingly burst-like and discontinuous.
\n\n
NC框架给出了有限的Hawking温度，当黑洞质量趋近于最小残余质量时温度趋于零，从而消除了通常的发散。熵获得对数修正：$S = \frac{A}{4G} + \alpha \ln\left(\frac{A}{4G}\right) + \cdots$。总辐射粒子数与初始熵成正比，证实了其非热特性。关键的是，稀疏性参数满足$\hat{\eta} \gg 1$，且在蒸发末期发散（$\hat{\eta} \to \infty$），表明辐射变得越来越呈脉冲状且不连续。

Conclusion: Non-commutative gravity significantly alters black hole evaporation: it regularizes thermodynamic quantities, enforces a non-thermal and sparse emission profile, and implies that the final stage of evaporation is not a violent explosion but an asymptotic halt with diverging sparsity. This supports the idea that quantum spacetime structure suppresses high-energy radiation and may leave stable Planck-scale remnants.
\n\n
非对易引力显著改变了黑洞蒸发过程：它正则化了热力学量，强制辐射具有非热且稀疏的特性，并表明蒸发末期并非剧烈爆炸，而是以发散的稀疏性渐近停止。这支持了量子时空结构抑制高能辐射并可能留下稳定的Planck尺度残余物的观点。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the sparsity of black hole radiation, the Bekenstein entropy loss, and the total particle emission of a Schwarzschild black hole (SBH) within the framework of non-commutative (NC) gauge theory of gravity. First, we provide a brief review of black hole (BH) thermodynamics, computing both the deformed Hawking temperature and entropy. In this geometry, the divergent behavior of the temperature is removed, and a logarithmic correction to the entropy emerges. We then present the NC corrections to the Bekenstein entropy loss of the SBH alongside the total number of emitted particles. Our results show that the total number of emitted particles is proportional to the entropy behavior of the NC SBH, which is consistent with non-thermal radiation. Finally, we analyze the sparsity of Hawking radiation in this geometry, finding that the NC SBH exhibits extremely sparse radiation ($\hatη \gg 1$), which diverges at the final stage of evaporation as the black hole ceases to radiate.

</details>


### [53] [Gotta light? Illuminating AGN disks with LISA EMRIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12531)
*Federico Fantocolli,Francisco Duque,Jonathan Gair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: LISA can simultaneously constrain the accretion disk surface density and accretion rate of EMRIs using relativistic binary-disk torque models in a Bayesian framework, achieving ~10% precision on torque amplitude without needing electromagnetic counterparts; Fisher matrix methods are inadequate for such analyses.
\n\n
LISA 利用相对论性双星-吸积盘扭矩模型和贝叶斯框架，可在无需电磁对应体的情况下同时约束 EMRI 吸积盘的面密度和吸积率（或总光度），对扭矩振幅的约束精度可达约 10%；Fisher 矩阵方法在此类系统中不适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Previous forecasts for LISA’s ability to probe gas torques on EMRIs relied on Newtonian models, which may not capture strong-field relativistic effects near massive black holes. Moreover, it was unclear whether LISA alone—without electromagnetic counterparts—could break degeneracies between disk properties like surface density and accretion rate. This paper addresses these gaps by employing fully relativistic binary-disk interaction models and a rigorous Bayesian inference framework.
\n\n
先前对 LISA 探测 EMRI 上气体扭矩能力的预测依赖于牛顿力学模型，可能无法捕捉大质量黑洞附近强场相对论效应。此外，尚不清楚在没有电磁对应体的情况下，LISA 是否能单独打破吸积盘面密度与吸积率等参数之间的简并性。本文通过采用完全相对论性的双星-吸积盘相互作用模型和严格的贝叶斯推断框架来解决这些问题。

Method: The authors employ recently developed relativistic models of binary-disk interactions to compute gas torques on EMRIs embedded in accretion disks. They perform full Bayesian parameter estimation (not relying on Fisher matrix approximations) to infer posterior distributions for disk parameters—specifically surface density $\\Sigma$ and accretion rate $\\dot{M}$ (or total luminosity)—using simulated LISA data for typical EMRI waveforms modified by gas torques.
\n\n
作者采用最新发展的相对论性双星-吸积盘相互作用模型，计算嵌入吸积盘中的 EMRI 所受气体扭矩。他们利用模拟的 LISA 数据，对受气体扭矩调制的典型 EMRI 波形进行完整的贝叶斯参数估计（而非依赖 Fisher 矩阵近似），以推断吸积盘参数——特别是面密度 $\\Sigma$ 和吸积率 $\\dot{M}$（或总光度）——的后验分布。

Result: The analysis shows that LISA can simultaneously constrain both the disk surface density and accretion rate with no need for electromagnetic counterparts. The torque amplitude is measurable to within ~10% for typical EMRI observations. Crucially, the study finds that the linear-signal (Fisher matrix) approximation fails for these systems, underscoring the necessity of full Bayesian inference. These measurements probe accretion physics at (sub)microparsec scales in the strong-gravity regime.
\n\n
分析表明，LISA 可在无需电磁对应体的情况下同时约束吸积盘面密度和吸积率。对于典型的 EMRI 观测，扭矩振幅可被测量到约 10% 的精度。关键的是，研究发现线性信号（Fisher 矩阵）近似在此类系统中失效，凸显了完整贝叶斯推断的必要性。这些测量可在（亚）微秒差距尺度上探测强引力场中的吸积物理。

Conclusion: LISA will be capable of probing accretion disk physics around massive black holes through gravitational-wave-only observations of EMRIs, offering unique insights into black hole growth, galaxy coevolution, and enabling EMRIs to serve as cosmological (dark) sirens when cross-correlated with AGN catalogs.
\n\n
LISA 将能够仅通过 EMRI 的引力波观测来探测大质量黑洞周围的吸积盘物理，为黑洞增长、星系协同演化提供独特见解，并可通过与活动星系核（AGN）星表交叉关联，使 EMRI 成为宇宙学（暗）标准汽笛。

Abstract: We study the ability of the upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) to constrain gas torques acting on extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) when these are embedded in accretion disks, using recently developed relativistic models for the binary-disk interaction. Using a fully Bayesian setup, we find that, contrary to previous forecasts based on Newtonian results, these observations can provide simultaneous estimates of the disk surface density and the accretion rate (or, equivalently, its total luminosity) without the need for an electromagnetic counterpart. Our analysis also indicates that simpler measurement constraints based on the linear-signal (Fisher matrix) approximation are not valid for these systems. For typical EMRI observations, the torque amplitude can be constrained to within ~10%, strengthening the prospect of probing accretion physics at (sub)microparsec scales, deep in the strong-field gravity regime and complementing electromagnetic observations. This also strengthens LISA's ability to help answering questions such as how massive black holes grow and coevolve with their host galaxies and, by helping to identify the EMRI's host galaxy through cross-correlation with AGN catalogues, to improve the use of these sources as (dark) sirens for cosmology.

</details>


### [54] [Maxwell equations in Schwarzschild spacetime for static and freely falling observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12630)
*F. L. Carneiro,L. V. A. Cunha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: The paper reformulates classical electrodynamics in Schwarzschild spacetime using a tetrad (orthonormal frame) approach to maintain observer-dependent physical interpretations of electromagnetic fields. It compares Maxwell's equations as seen by static and radially free-falling observers, revealing gravitational corrections for static observers and kinematical mixing of electric and magnetic sectors for free-falling ones. The results are interpreted via an effective-medium analogy, where spacetime geometry mimics an inhomogeneous medium—static for stationary observers and radially flowing for infalling observers.
\n\n
本文在Schwarzschild时空中采用tetrad（正交标架）方法重新表述经典电动力学，以保持电磁场对观测者的直接物理解释。文章比较了静态观测者与径向自由下落观测者所见的Maxwell方程：前者因引力场引入几何修正，后者则因局部径向boost导致电荷密度与径向电流混合、电场与磁场耦合。结果通过有效介质类比进行解释——对静态观测者，时空几何表现为静止的非均匀介质；对自由下落观测者，则表现为在时间-径向平面中径向流动的有效介质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to clarify how classical electromagnetic phenomena are perceived by different classes of observers in curved spacetime, particularly in Schwarzschild geometry. Standard coordinate-based formulations obscure the direct physical meaning of $\\mathbf{E}$ and $\\mathbf{B}$ fields. A tetrad formalism restores this interpretation by aligning field components with local inertial frames carried by observers. This enables a physically transparent analysis of how gravity and motion affect Maxwell’s equations.
\n\n
动机在于澄清在弯曲时空中（特别是Schwarzschild几何中）不同类别的观测者如何感知经典电磁现象。基于坐标的常规表述会掩盖电场$\\mathbf{E}$和磁场$\\mathbf{B}$的直接物理意义。tetrad形式通过将场分量与观测者携带的局域惯性系对齐，恢复了这种物理解释，从而能清晰分析引力和运动如何影响Maxwell方程。

Method: The authors employ a tetrad (vierbein) formalism to project the spacetime metric onto orthonormal frames comoving with two types of observers: static observers (with worldlines tangent to the timelike Killing vector) and radially free-falling observers (following geodesics from rest at infinity). Maxwell’s equations are expressed in terms of the observer-adapted electric and magnetic fields defined via $F_{\\mu\\nu}$. The resulting 3+1 split yields modified Gauss, Faraday, Ampère-Maxwell, and no-monopole laws, incorporating both gravitational (metric function $f(r) = 1 - 2M/r$) and kinematical (local Lorentz boost) effects. The weak-field ($r \\gg 2M$) and near-horizon ($r \\to 2M$) limits are analyzed, and an effective-medium analogy is developed by comparing the modified Maxwell equations to those in macroscopic electrodynamics in material media.
\n\n
作者采用tetrad（vierbein）形式，将时空度规投影到两类观测者的正交标架上：静态观测者（世界线沿类时Killing矢量）和径向自由下落观测者（从无穷远静止开始沿测地线运动）。Maxwell方程通过观测者适配的电场和磁场（由$F_{\\mu\\nu}$定义）表达。所得3+1分解导出了修正的Gauss定律、Faraday定律、Ampère-Maxwell定律及无磁单极定律，其中包含引力效应（度规函数$f(r) = 1 - 2M/r$）和运动学效应（局域Lorentz boost）。文章分析了弱场极限（$r \\gg 2M$）和近视界极限（$r \\to 2M$），并通过将修正后的Maxwell方程与宏观电动力学中的介质方程类比，构建了有效介质图像。

Result: For static observers, Maxwell’s equations retain their standard spherical form but acquire geometric corrections through the lapse function $\\sqrt{f(r)}$, proper radial distance $dl = dr/\\sqrt{f(r)}$, and proper time $d\\tau = \\sqrt{f(r)} dt$. For radially free-falling observers, a local boost introduces off-diagonal terms in the temporal-radial sector: charge density $\\rho$ mixes with radial current $j^r$, and the electric and magnetic fields couple in the $t$-$r$ components of Maxwell’s equations. However, the angular (transverse) Faraday and Ampère-Maxwell equations remain unchanged. In the effective-medium picture, static observers see spacetime as a dielectric-magnetic medium at rest with position-dependent permittivity and permeability, while free-falling observers perceive it as a moving medium with non-zero effective velocity in the radial direction.
\n\n
对静态观测者而言，Maxwell方程保持标准球对称形式，但通过lapse函数$\\sqrt{f(r)}$、固有径向距离$dl = dr/\\sqrt{f(r)}$和固有时$d\\tau = \\sqrt{f(r)} dt$引入了几何修正。对径向自由下落观测者，局域boost在时间-径向平面引入非对角项：电荷密度$\\rho$与径向电流$j^r$混合，且电场与磁场在Maxwell方程的$t$-$r$分量中耦合。然而，角向（横向）的Faraday和Ampère-Maxwell方程保持不变。在有效介质图像中，静态观测者看到的时空表现为静止的介电-磁性介质，其介电常数和磁导率随位置变化；而自由下落观测者则感知到一个在径向具有非零有效速度的运动介质。

Conclusion: The tetrad-based formulation provides a physically intuitive framework for electrodynamics in curved spacetime by linking field measurements directly to observer motion. Gravitational effects manifest either as geometric distortions of space and time (for static observers) or as kinematical couplings between electric and magnetic sectors (for free-falling observers). The effective-medium analogy offers a powerful heuristic: Schwarzschild geometry can be interpreted as an inhomogeneous optical medium whose properties depend on the observer’s state of motion, thereby unifying gravitational and electromagnetic responses in a classical setting.
\n\n
基于tetrad的表述通过将场测量直接关联到观测者运动，为弯曲时空中的电动力学提供了物理直观的框架。引力效应表现为：对静态观测者是时空的几何畸变，对自由下落观测者则是电场与磁场之间的运动学耦合。有效介质类比提供了一种强有力的启发式图像：Schwarzschild几何可被解释为一种非均匀光学介质，其性质依赖于观测者的运动状态，从而在经典框架下统一了引力与电磁响应。

Abstract: Classical electrodynamics in curved spacetime is formulated within a tetrad-based framework that preserves the direct physical interpretation of the electromagnetic fields measured by an observer. The formalism is applied to Schwarzschild spacetime for two distinct families of observers described in the same coordinate system: static observers and radially free-falling observers. For static observers, Maxwell equations retain their usual spherical structure, while the gravitational field introduces geometrical corrections in the radial and temporal sectors through the metric function. These corrections are interpreted in terms of proper radial distance, proper time, and the radial variation of the lapse function. For radially free-falling observers, additional kinematical contributions arise from the local radial boost relating the free-falling frame to the static one. As a consequence, charge density mixes with radial current, and the electric and magnetic sectors become intertwined in the temporal-radial projections of Maxwell equations, whereas the angular Ampère-Maxwell and Faraday equations retain the same structure found for the static observer. The weak-field and near-horizon regimes are also examined, and the results are discussed through an effective-medium analogy, in which the Schwarzschild geometry behaves as an inhomogeneous geometrical medium at rest for static observers and as a radially moving effective medium in the temporal-radial sector for free-falling observers.

### [55] [Higher Dimensional Loop Quantum Black hole in de Sitter Spacetime: Quasinormal Modes and Shadow Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12698)
*Kourosh Nozari,Sara Saghafi,Ali Mohammadpour*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies a higher-dimensional black hole solution incorporating loop quantum gravity corrections in a de Sitter background, analyzing its quasinormal modes (QNMs) and shadow. Three computational methods—time-domain evolution with Prony extraction, the matrix method, and WKB approximation—are used to compute QNMs of massless scalar fields, showing consistent results. Loop quantum corrections moderately shift the QNM spectrum, while spacetime dimensionality strongly increases oscillation frequencies and damping rates. All modes have negative imaginary parts, confirming dynamical stability. The black hole shadow radius is found to depend sensitively on the loop quantum parameter, cosmological constant, and number of dimensions, with extra dimensions shrinking the shadow. Observational EHT data for M87$^{\ast}$ are used to constrain the model’s parameter space, suggesting QNMs and shadows as complementary probes of quantum gravity and extra dimensions.

本文研究了在de Sitter背景中包含Loop Quantum Gravity（LQG）修正的高维黑洞解，分析其准正规模（Quasinormal Modes, QNMs）和黑洞阴影。作者采用三种计算方法——时域演化结合Prony提取、矩阵方法和WKB近似——计算无质量标量场的QNMs，结果高度一致。LQG修正对QNMs谱产生适度偏移，而时空维度对振荡频率和阻尼率的影响更为显著。所有模式的虚部均为负，表明在所研究参数范围内系统对无质量标量扰动是动力学稳定的。黑洞阴影半径对LQG参数、宇宙学常数和时空维度数目敏感，额外维度通常会缩小阴影尺寸。通过与M87$^{\ast}$的Event Horizon Telescope（EHT）观测数据对比，获得了模型参数空间的限制，表明QNMs和阴影可作为探测量子引力效应和高维时空结构的互补探针。
