  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to explore observational signatures of quantum gravity and extra spatial dimensions in strong-field gravity regimes. Specifically, the authors aim to understand how loop quantum corrections and higher-dimensional spacetime affect two key observables: quasinormal modes (probing dynamical response) and black hole shadow (probing optical/geometric structure). By confronting theoretical predictions with current astrophysical data (e.g., EHT), they seek to constrain or test extensions of general relativity involving loop quantum gravity and extra dimensions.
\n\n
动机在于探索量子引力和额外空间维度在强引力场中的可观测信号。具体而言，作者旨在理解Loop Quantum Gravity修正和高维时空如何影响两个关键观测量：准正规模（反映动力学响应）和黑洞阴影（反映光学/几何结构）。通过将理论预言与当前天体物理观测数据（如Event Horizon Telescope）进行对比，试图对包含LQG和额外维度的广义相对论扩展模型施加限制或检验。

Method: The authors employ three independent numerical/semi-analytical techniques to compute quasinormal modes of massless scalar perturbations: (1) time-domain integration of the perturbation equation followed by Prony method for frequency extraction, (2) the matrix method based on spectral decomposition, and (3) higher-order WKB approximation. For the optical analysis, they solve the null geodesic equations in the higher-dimensional loop-quantum-corrected de Sitter black hole spacetime to determine the photon sphere and compute the shadow radius as observed at infinity. They then compare this theoretical shadow radius with the measured size of M87$^{\ast}$ from the Event Horizon Telescope to derive bounds on the loop quantum parameter and the number of spacetime dimensions.
\n\n
作者采用三种独立的数值/半解析方法计算无质量标量扰动的准正规模：(1) 扰动方程的时域积分结合Prony方法提取频率；(2) 基于谱分解的矩阵方法；(3) 高阶WKB近似。在光学分析方面，他们在高维Loop Quantum修正的de Sitter黑洞时空中求解零测地线方程，确定光子球面，并计算无穷远处观测到的阴影半径。随后将该理论阴影半径与Event Horizon Telescope对M87$^{\ast}$的测量结果进行比较，从而对LQG参数和时空维度数目施加限制。

Result: The three methods for computing quasinormal modes show excellent agreement. Loop quantum corrections cause moderate shifts in the real and imaginary parts of the modes, while increasing spacetime dimension $d$ significantly raises both the oscillation frequency ($\\text{Re}(\\omega)$) and damping rate ($|\\text{Im}(\\omega)|$). All computed modes satisfy $\\text{Im}(\\omega) < 0$, confirming linear stability against scalar perturbations. The shadow radius decreases with larger loop quantum parameter, larger cosmological constant, and higher spacetime dimension. Using EHT constraints on M87$^{\ast}$ shadow size, the authors exclude regions of the $(d, \\text{loop parameter})$ parameter space, particularly ruling out large extra dimensions for certain quantum gravity scales.
\n\n
三种准正规模计算方法结果高度一致。Loop Quantum修正对模式实部和虚部造成适度偏移，而增加时空维度$d$显著提高振荡频率（$\\text{Re}(\\omega)$）和阻尼率（$|\\text{Im}(\\omega)|$）。所有计算出的模式均满足$\\text{Im}(\\omega) < 0$，证实了对标量扰动的线性稳定性。阴影半径随LQG参数增大、宇宙学常数增大以及时空维度升高而减小。利用EHT对M87$^{\ast}$阴影尺寸的限制，作者排除了$(d, \\text{LQG参数})$参数空间中的某些区域，特别是对特定量子引力能标下较大的额外维度予以排除。

Conclusion: Quasinormal modes and black hole shadow provide robust and complementary observables to probe quantum gravitational effects and extra dimensions in the strong-field regime. The consistency across multiple computational methods strengthens confidence in the results. The dependence of both QNMs and shadow on the loop quantum parameter and spacetime dimension allows joint constraints from gravitational-wave ringdown and very-long-baseline interferometry. Future multi-messenger observations could tightly constrain or detect signatures of loop quantum gravity and higher-dimensional physics near black holes.
\n\n
准正规模和黑洞阴影是在强场区域探测量子引力效应和额外维度的稳健且互补的观测量。多种计算方法的一致性增强了结果的可信度。QNMs和阴影对LQG参数及时空维度的依赖性使得可通过引力波ringdown信号和甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）联合施加限制。未来的多信使观测有望对黑洞附近的Loop Quantum Gravity和高维物理效应施加严格限制或实现探测。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical and optical properties of a higher-dimensional loop-quantum-corrected black hole in a de Sitter background. The quasinormal modes of massless scalar perturbations are computed using time-domain evolution with Prony extraction, the matrix method, and the WKB approximation, showing good agreement among the three approaches. We find that loop quantum corrections induce moderate shifts in the quasinormal spectrum, whereas the spacetime dimensionality has a much stronger impact, leading to higher oscillation frequencies and damping rates. The negative imaginary parts of all modes indicate dynamical stability against massless scalar perturbations within the explored parameter range. We also analyze null geodesics and construct the corresponding black hole shadow. The shadow radius depends sensitively on the loop quantum parameter, the cosmological constant, and the number of spacetime dimensions, with extra dimensions generally reducing the shadow size. By comparing the theoretical shadow radius with the Event Horizon Telescope constraints for M87$^{\ast}$, we obtain bounds on the parameter space of the model. These results suggest that quasinormal modes and black hole shadow observables can provide complementary probes of loop quantum gravity effects and higher-dimensional spacetime structure in the strong-gravity regime.

</details>


### [56] [Asymmetric quantum steering harvested near a Lorentz-violating BTZ black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12766)
*Si-Yu Liu,Xin-Ze Song,Xiang-Yue Yu,Wentao Liu,Xiao-Li Huang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper studies quantum steering between two Unruh-DeWitt detectors in a Lorentz-violating BTZ black hole spacetime, revealing that the detector exposed to higher effective thermal noise (due to gravitational redshift) can exhibit stronger steerability—a reversal of standard thermal intuition. Steering exists only within a finite range of detector energy gaps, peaks at an optimal gap, and is most suppressed by Lorentz violation near this optimum, showing that Lorentz symmetry breaking geometrically constrains both the magnitude and directionality of quantum correlations.
\n\n
本文研究了Lorentz对称性破缺的BTZ黑洞时空中两个Unruh-DeWitt探测器之间的quantum steering，揭示出由于gravitational redshift导致处于更高有效热噪声环境中的探测器反而展现出更强的steerability——这与标准热力学直觉相反。Steering仅在探测器能隙的有限窗口内存在，并在最优能隙处达到峰值；而Lorentz violation在此最优能隙附近对steering的抑制最强，表明Lorentz对称性破缺作为一种几何约束，同时限制了quantum correlations的强度和方向性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding how quantum correlations—specifically quantum steering and its directional asymmetry—are affected by the interplay of strong gravity, thermal effects due to gravitational redshift, and fundamental symmetry breaking (Lorentz violation) in a curved spacetime background like the BTZ black hole. The authors aim to test whether conventional thermal intuition about decoherence holds in such settings and to explore the role of spacetime symmetries in enabling or restricting quantum information protocols.
\n\n
动机源于理解quantum correlations（特别是quantum steering及其方向不对称性）如何受到强引力、gravitational redshift引起的热效应以及基本对称性破缺（Lorentz violation）在BTZ黑洞这类弯曲时空背景中的共同影响。作者旨在检验在该类环境中，关于退相干的传统热力学直觉是否依然成立，并探索spacetime symmetries在允许或限制quantum information协议中的作用。

Method: The authors model two spatially separated Unruh-DeWitt detectors coupled to a quantum field in a Lorentz-violating extension of the BTZ black hole geometry. They compute the Gaussian quantum steering between the detectors using their joint covariance matrix derived from the field’s Wightman function, accounting for differing local temperatures induced by gravitational redshift. The analysis includes varying the detector energy gap $\\Omega$ and the Lorentz-violating parameter, then evaluating the Gaussian steering asymmetry $\\mathcal{G}^{A \\to B}$ vs. $\\mathcal{G}^{B \\to A}$.
\n\n
作者构建了两个空间分离的Unruh-DeWitt探测器，它们耦合到Lorentz-violating修正的BTZ黑洞几何中的量子场。通过场的Wightman函数计算探测器联合协方差矩阵，并考虑gravitational redshift导致的不同局部温度，进而计算Gaussian quantum steering。分析中变化探测器能隙$\\Omega$和Lorentz-violating参数，并评估Gaussian steering不对称性$\\mathcal{G}^{A \\to B}$与$\\mathcal{G}^{B \\to A}$。

Result: The key results are: (1) Alice, who experiences a higher effective temperature due to being closer to the black hole, exhibits stronger steerability toward Bob than vice versa—contradicting naive thermal expectations; (2) quantum steering is nonzero only for a finite interval of energy gaps $\\Omega$, with a clear maximum at an optimal $\\Omega_{\\text{opt}}$; (3) Lorentz violation most strongly suppresses steering precisely near $\\Omega_{\\text{opt}}$, indicating maximal sensitivity of quantum correlations to symmetry breaking at peak performance; (4) both the magnitude and directionality of steering are constrained by Lorentz violation, implying a geometric limitation on spacetime’s quantum information capacity.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 由于更靠近黑洞而经历更高有效温度的Alice对Bob的steerability强于反向情况——与朴素热力学预期相悖；(2) quantum steering仅在有限的能隙区间$\\Omega$内非零，并在最优值$\\Omega_{\\text{opt}}$处达到最大值；(3) Lorentz violation恰好在$\\Omega_{\\text{opt}}$附近对steering的抑制最强，表明quantum correlations在性能峰值处对symmetry breaking最为敏感；(4) Lorentz violation同时约束steering的强度和方向性，意味着spacetime的quantum information capacity存在几何限制。

Conclusion: The study concludes that in curved spacetimes with Lorentz violation, the extraction of directional quantum correlations is fundamentally limited by geometric and symmetry-breaking effects. The inversion of thermal intuition highlights the nontrivial role of gravity in quantum information, while the enhanced suppression near optimal correlation regimes reveals that maximal quantum resources are most vulnerable to deviations from fundamental symmetries. Thus, Lorentz violation acts as a geometric regulator of quantum steering, shaping both its strength and asymmetry.
\n\n
本研究得出结论：在具有Lorentz violation的弯曲时空中，directional quantum correlations的提取从根本上受到几何结构和symmetry-breaking效应的限制。热力学直觉的反转凸显了引力在quantum information中的非平凡作用，而最优关联区域附近增强的抑制效应表明，最大化的quantum resources对基本对称性的偏离最为敏感。因此，Lorentz violation作为一种几何调节器，塑造了quantum steering的强度和不对称性。

Abstract: We investigate the harvesting of quantum steering and its directional asymmetry between two Unruh-DeWitt detectors in a Lorentz-violating BTZ black hole spacetime. Since the detectors are located at different radial positions outside the black hole, they experience inequivalent local environments induced by gravitational redshift, causing Alice to undergo stronger effective thermal noise than Bob. Remarkably, we uncover a counterintuitive phenomenon in which the detector subjected to a higher effective temperature exhibits stronger steerability than the other one, revealing a nontrivial inversion of thermal intuition in curved spacetime. Furthermore, quantum steering survives only within a finite window of detector energy gaps and reaches its maximum within an optimal regime. We find that Lorentz violation suppresses steering most strongly near this optimal energy gap, indicating an enhanced sensitivity of maximal correlation extraction to symmetry breaking effects. Our results demonstrate that Lorentz violation acts as a geometric constraint on the quantum information capacity of spacetime, simultaneously restricting both the strength and the directionality of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [57] [Constraining Kerr supermassive black hole properties using gravitational waves from inspiraling stellar-mass binary black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12784)
*Jie Wu,Jin-Tao Yao,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: Future space-based gravitational-wave detectors like LISA can precisely measure supermassive black hole (SMBH) properties—such as mass, spin magnitude, and orientation—by observing modulated signals from inspiraling stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) in stable hierarchical triples. Relative uncertainties for SMBH mass can reach $10^{-5}$, and spin parameters can be constrained to a few percent, surpassing current electromagnetic measurements for systems like M87*.
\n\n
未来的空间引力波探测器（如LISA）可通过观测稳定分层三体系统中旋进的恒星级双黑洞（BBH）产生的调制信号，精确测量超大质量黑洞（SMBH）的质量、自旋大小和方向等性质。SMBH质量的相对不确定度可达$10^{-5}$量级，自旋参数可被限制在百分之几以内，优于当前对M87*类系统的电磁观测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current electromagnetic observations of SMBHs, such as those of M87*, provide limited precision on mass and especially spin parameters. Space-based gravitational-wave astronomy offers a complementary and potentially more precise probe of SMBH spacetime geometry through the relativistic modulation of GW signals from orbiting stellar-mass BBHs, motivating a detailed study of parameter estimation capabilities with future detectors like LISA.
\n\n
当前对超大质量黑洞（如M87*）的电磁观测在质量和尤其是自旋参数上的精度有限。空间引力波天文学通过轨道上恒星级双黑洞（BBH）引力波信号的相对论性调制，可提供一种互补且可能更精确的SMBH时空几何探针，这促使人们详细研究LISA等未来探测器的参数估计能力。

Method: The authors model the motion of a stellar-mass BBH in the Kerr spacetime of a central SMBH within a stable hierarchical triple configuration. They compute the resulting modulated GW waveform by combining post-Newtonian (PN) approximations for the inner BBH inspiral with a moving-source transformation accounting for the outer orbit around the SMBH. Parameter estimation is performed using the Fisher information matrix, assuming the LISA detector configuration and second-generation time-delay interferometry (TDI).
\n\n
作者在稳定分层三体构型下，将恒星级双黑洞（BBH）的运动建模为绕中心SMBH的Kerr时空中的轨道运动。他们通过结合内轨道BBH旋进的后牛顿（post-Newtonian, PN）近似与考虑外轨道运动的移动源变换（moving-source transformation），计算由此产生的调制引力波波形。参数估计采用Fisher信息矩阵方法，并假设LISA探测器构型及第二代时延干涉仪（TDI）技术。

Result: The outer orbital semimajor axis dominates the precision of parameter estimation. For high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) events, the SMBH mass can be measured with relative uncertainty $\sim 10^{-5}$, while the spin magnitude and orientation are constrained to within a few percent. The SMBH spin and eccentricity primarily influence their own measurement uncertainties. When applied to an M87*-like system, GW-based measurements outperform current electromagnetic constraints on both mass and spin.
\n\n
外轨道半长轴对参数估计精度起主导作用。对于高信噪比（SNR）事件，SMBH质量的相对不确定度可达$\sim 10^{-5}$，而自旋大小和方向可被限制在百分之几以内。SMBH自旋和偏心率主要影响其自身测量的不确定性。应用于M87*类系统时，基于引力波的测量在质量和自旋参数上均优于当前的电磁约束。

Conclusion: Space-based gravitational-wave detectors such as LISA will enable unprecedentedly precise measurements of SMBH properties by observing relativistically modulated GWs from inspiraling stellar-mass BBHs in hierarchical triples. This method provides a powerful new avenue to test general relativity in the strong-field regime and map SMBH demographics with high fidelity.

For high-SNR signals, the SMBH mass and orbital parameters can be measured with relative uncertainties on the order of $10^{-5}$, while the spin magnitude and its orientation can be constrained to within a few percentages. Abstract: Gravitational wave (GW) observations provide an unprecedented laboratory for testing general relativity (GR) in the strong-field, highly dynamic, and relativistic regimes. Within the parameterized post-Newtonian (PN) formalisms, waveform generation tests have conventionally been limited to constraining inspiral coefficients up to the 3.5PN order. Leveraging the recent theoretical breakthrough that extended the analytical compact binary phasing to the 4.5PN order, we present the first observational constraints on these higher-order effects. Our analysis utilizes two exceptional events detected by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) network: GW250114\_082203, which boasts the highest signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) recorded to date, and GW230627\_015337, which features a uniquely prolonged inspiral phase and the highest inspiral phase SNR to date. By performing Bayesian inference on the dimensionless deviation parameters ($δφ_i$) associated with the 4PN and 4.5PN coefficients, we find that our results are fully consistent with the predictions of GR. While the current 90\% credible intervals for the four deviation parameters are of order $\mathcal{O}(1) \text{-} \mathcal{O}(10)$, the general relativistic null values ($δ\hatφ_a= 0$) are entirely encapsulated within the bounds. R. L. {\bf 130}, 011402 (2023)].

Thermodynamics is studied in the extended phase space where $\Lambda$ is treated as a thermodynamic pressure $P = -\\Lambda/(8\\pi)$, and the Gibbs free energy $G = M - TS$ is compared between black hole and thermal-AdS phases to locate Hawking–Page transitions.

Specifically, for large AdS radius $\\ell = 1/\\sqrt{-\\Lambda}$, de Sitter-core black holes have higher $T_{\\text{HP}}$ than AdS-core ones; however, this ordering can reverse near the minimal allowed $\ell$, indicating that thermodynamic differences arise not merely from regularity but from how the core deformation reshapes the horizon branch relative to the thermal-AdS background.

作者成功构建了在渐近AdS时空中具有de Sitter或AdS核心的正则黑洞解族。他们证明这些模型满足广义Birkhoff定理，并具有明确定义的外视界。在扩展热力学相空间中，主导相变仍为Hawking–Page型。正则化通过改变外视界分支$r_+(M)$（包括其端点结构）来修正相变温度$T_{\\text{HP}}$的具体数值。具体而言，当AdS半径$\ell = 1/\\sqrt{-\\Lambda}$较大时，de Sitter核心黑洞的$T_{\\text{HP}}$高于AdS核心黑洞；但在$\ell$接近允许下限时，该顺序可能反转，表明热力学差异不仅源于正则性本身，更源于核心形变如何相对于thermal-AdS背景重塑视界分支。

Conclusion: The study shows that effective loop quantum gravity-inspired regularizations can yield physically consistent black hole solutions in asymptotically AdS spacetimes that preserve key thermodynamic features like the Hawking–Page transition. Crucially, the thermodynamic behavior is sensitive not just to the presence of a regular core, but to its specific geometric nature (de Sitter vs. AdS), which influences the global horizon structure. 研究表明，受圈量子引力启发的有效正则化可在渐近AdS时空中产生物理自洽的黑洞解，并保留Hawking–Page相变等关键热力学特征。关键在于，热力学行为不仅对正则核心的存在敏感，更对其具体几何性质（de Sitter vs. AdS）敏感，后者影响全局视界结构。这意味着编码于核心的量子引力效应可在黑洞热力学中留下可观测印记，尤其在全息背景下，此类相变对偶于规范理论中的禁闭/退禁闭相变。

Abstract: We derive a class of vacuum regular black holes inspired by effective loop quantum gravity dynamics and extend the construction to asymptotically anti-de Sitter spacetimes. The derivation is based on a deparameterized Lemaître--Tolman--Bondi formulation, where an auxiliary dust field is introduced only to define an internal time and does not act as a matter source. In spherical symmetry, the dynamics reduces to a set of independent radial shells, giving rise to a factorized shell Hamiltonian and to a Birkhoff-type property: for a fixed reconstruction function and cosmological constant, the static geometry is uniquely determined by the mass. We then study their thermodynamics in the extended phase space, with particular emphasis on the Hawking--Page transition. The regularization affects the quantitative transition temperature by deforming the physical outer-horizon branch, including its endpoint structure. In the large anti-de Sitter radius regime, the de Sitter core solutions exhibit a higher Hawking--Page temperature than their anti-de Sitter-core counterparts, while the ordering can be modified close to the lower admissible range of the AdS scale. Thus, the thermodynamic differences between the two classes are not a consequence of regularity alone, but arise from how the core deformation modifies the horizon branch relative to the thermal-AdS reference background.

Abstract: In the present work, we study the absorption cross section of a Schwarzschild black hole for a massive vector field over arbitrary frequencies. We then numerically compute these quantities over arbitrary frequencies. In contrast to the massless (photon) case, massive vector bosons exhibit new features arising from the field mass. Furthermore, it leads to a scalar-type branch in the even parity transverse modes, thereby breaking the usual degeneracy with the odd parity sector found in the massless case. These results show that any such spindle deformation, if extended to the solar exterior geometry, must be extremely suppressed on Solar-System scales.

  <summary>Details</summary>

宇宙学常数$Λ$虽主导大尺度宇宙加速膨胀，但可能在局域引力动力学中留下微弱印记。在银河系中心这一强相对论区域检验其影响，为超越宇宙学观测的基本物理提供了独特探针。此前研究缺乏结合多颗恒星轨道并完整处理相对论效应的联合分析。

Abstract: We constrain the cosmological constant $Λ$ using astrometric and spectroscopic observations of the S2, S1, and S14 stars orbiting Sgr A$^*$. 本文通过概念分析和案例研究（而非形式化计算）展开论证。它考察了四个典型案例——伦德勒视界与安鲁效应、黑洞热力学与雅各布森对爱因斯坦方程的状态方程推导、引力波探测，以及外尔引力与共形引力——以说明对几何的经验获取如何依赖于动力学稳定性、因果结构和尺度固定。文章进一步结合涌现引力和量子参考系等框架讨论其含义。

Result: The analysis reveals that in quantum gravity scenarios where geometry is not fundamental, the recovery of general relativity is incomplete unless the physical preconditions for objective measurement are also satisfied. 分析表明，在几何非基本的量子引力情形中，除非同时满足客观测量的物理前提条件，否则广义相对论的恢复是不完整的。特别是，共形引力作为一个关键反例：其尺度不变性阻碍了时空尺度的绝对固定，除非该对称性被破缺或辅以额外的物理结构，否则将损害经验意义。

Conclusion: The paper concludes that a general epistemological constraint applies to all approaches to quantum gravity: beyond reproducing geometric dynamics, a theory must guarantee the physical possibility of stable, causally connected, and invariant reference systems that enable objective determination of relational geometrical quantities. This bridges the gap between formal recovery of GR and its empirical content.

本文得出结论：所有量子引力方法都必须满足一个普遍的认识论约束：除了重现几何动力学之外，理论还必须保证稳定、因果关联且描述不变的参考系统的物理可能性，以实现对关系性几何量的客观确定。这弥合了广义相对论的形式恢复与其经验内容之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This paper develops an epistemological constraint on quantum gravity grounded in the empirical meaning of general relativity. The central claim is that a complete recovery of general relativity requires an effective metric, a continuum limit, or Einstein-like dynamics together with the physical conditions under which relational geometrical quantities can be objectively determined. These conditions concern the dynamical stability of measuring devices and reference systems, causal accessibility among physical systems, record formation, and invariance under admissible descriptions. In classical general relativity, they are usually implicit in the use of clocks, rods, light signals, freely falling bodies, detectors, and gauge-invariant observables. In quantum gravity, however, they become non-trivial because spacetime geometry may be emergent, effective, thermodynamic, relational, or frame-dependent. This claim is developed through four cases: Rindler horizons and the Unruh effect, black-hole thermodynamics and Jacobson's equation-of-state derivation, gravitational-wave detection, and Weyl and conformal gravity. The latter is discussed as a critical limiting case in which conformal invariance raises a sharp question about whether scale-dependent measurements of space and time can be physically fixed. Implications for quantum gravity are also discussed using emergent gravity and quantum reference frames as examples. The perspective developed in the study suggests a general epistemological constraint on quantum gravity: any viable approach must recover the physical possibility of objective geometrical measurement together with geometry itself.

</details>


### [67] [Trapped Surface as a Cosmic Censor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13592)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The weak cosmic censorship conjecture (WCCC) aims to prevent the formation of naked singularities from generic gravitational collapse. The authors seek a more robust, local, and geometrically intrinsic criterion based on causal structure and trapped surfaces, which are fundamental in singularity theorems.

弱宇宙监督猜想（WCCC）旨在防止在一般引力坍缩中形成裸奇点。传统表述通常依赖于诸如渐近荷守恒或精细调节的极值极限等全局假设。作者寻求一种更稳健、局域且几何内蕴的判据，该判据基于因果结构和俘获面——后者是奇点定理中的基本要素。

Method: The method uses the null convergence condition ($R_{\mu\nu}k^\mu k^\nu \geq 0$ for null $k^\mu$) and the generic condition to show that injecting matter across a black hole horizon necessarily converts a horizon cross-section into a closed trapped surface. If candidate final geometries (e.g., superextremal Reissner-Nordström) lack such surfaces due to their causal structure, they are excluded as endpoints.

该方法利用零收敛条件（对零矢量 $k^\mu$ 满足 $R_{\mu\nu}k^\mu k^\nu \geq 0$）和非退化条件，证明物质注入黑洞视界必然将视界截面转变为闭合俘获面。随后应用拓扑监督定理和俘获面的性质：任何物理上可接受的最终时空必须包含该俘获面。若候选的最终几何（如超极值Reissner-Nordström解）因其因果结构而无法容纳此类曲面，则被排除为可能的终态。

Result: The trapped surface criterion successfully rules out superextremal Reissner-Nordström, Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter, Kerr-Newman black holes, and Weyl-class naked singularities as possible outcomes of overcharging or overspinning processes. Conclusion: Weak cosmic censorship can be enforced via a purely local and geometric condition rooted in the inevitability of trapped surface formation under energy conditions. 弱宇宙监督可通过一个纯粹局域且几何化的条件来实现，该条件根植于在能量条件下俘获面形成的必然性。这为该猜想提供了更强、更普适的基础，不依赖于全局对称性、渐近结构或精细调节的初值。

Abstract: We formulate a local geometric criterion for weak cosmic censorship in black hole overcharging and overspinning thought experiments. Under the null convergence and generic conditions, matter injection turns a horizon cross section into a closed trapped surface. Any final spacetime unable to accommodate this surface is ruled out. This trapped surface criterion excludes superextremal Reissner-Nordström, Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter, and Kerr-Newman final states, as well as Weyl-class naked singularities. Our criterion does not rely on asymptotic charges or on an extremal condition characterizing naked singularities.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the pathological ultraviolet behavior of the vacuum solution in lower-dimensional EGB gravity, which develops a genuine curvature singularity at the origin—contrary to the regular BTZ black hole in 2+1 dimensions. This raises the question of whether and how matter fields can be engineered to restore spacetime regularity while preserving asymptotic BTZ structure, thereby offering insights into quantum gravity effects, singularity resolution, and the role of higher-curvature terms.
\n\n
动机源于低维EGB引力中真空解在紫外区域的病态行为——其在原点处出现真实的曲率奇点，这与2+1维中正则的BTZ黑洞形成鲜明对比。这引发了一个关键问题：能否以及如何构造物质场以恢复时空正则性，同时保持BTZ渐近结构，从而为量子引力效应、奇点消除及高阶曲率项的作用提供新见解。

Method: The authors employ three complementary approaches: (1) solving the EGB field equations coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics to derive electromagnetic Lagrangians that yield regular black holes; (2) constructing Simpson–Visser black-bounce metrics with smooth throats and finite curvature invariants; and (3) formulating a unimodular version of lower-dimensional EGB gravity where a spacetime-dependent cosmological function dynamically mediates between vacuum and Maxwell matter sectors. Thermodynamic analysis is performed via standard black-hole thermodynamics adapted to these regular geometries.
\n\n
作者采用三种互补方法：(1) 求解耦合非线性电动力学的EGB场方程，直接导出能产生正则黑洞的电磁Lagrangian；(2) 构造具有光滑喉部和有限曲率不变量的Simpson–Visser黑洞-弹跳度规；(3) 建立低维EGB引力的unimodular推广形式，其中依赖时空的宇宙学函数动态地在真空与Maxwell物质之间进行交换。热力学分析通过适用于这些正则几何的标准黑洞热力学方法完成。

Result: The paper obtains explicit regular black-hole and black-bounce solutions in lower-dimensional EGB gravity with finite curvature invariants everywhere. 论文在低维EGB引力中获得了处处具有有限曲率不变量的显式正则黑洞与黑洞-弹跳解。非线性电动力学支持无奇点黑洞；黑洞-弹跳几何展现无奇点的光滑拓扑转变；unimodular框架允许标准Maxwell场生成正则时空。在热力学上，蒸发过程在有限质量处停止（形成残余），出现非平凡相变，且当弹跳参数趋于零时，黑洞-弹跳的热力学量平滑过渡到EGB-BTZ极限。

Conclusion: Lower-dimensional EGB gravity, despite its pathological vacuum solution, serves as a tractable theoretical laboratory for studying regular compact objects. The interplay between higher-curvature corrections, matter model engineering (nonlinear electrodynamics), geometric deformations (black bounces), and alternative gravitational formulations (unimodular gravity) enables singularity resolution and rich thermodynamic phenomena, highlighting its utility in exploring quantum-gravity-motivated modifications of general relativity.

尽管低维EGB引力的真空解存在病态行为，但它仍可作为研究正则致密天体的有效理论实验室。高阶曲率修正、物质模型构造（非线性电动力学）、几何形变（黑洞-弹跳）以及替代表述（unimodular引力）之间的相互作用，实现了奇点消除并展现出丰富的热力学现象，凸显了该框架在探索受量子引力启发的广义相对论修正中的价值。

Abstract: We investigate the construction of regular compact objects in the recently proposed lower-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet (EGB) gravity obtained through regularized dimensional reduction. Unlike the standard BTZ black hole, the corresponding vacuum EGB solution develops a genuine curvature singularity at the origin, providing an interesting setting in which higher-curvature corrections deteriorate the ultraviolet behavior of spacetime. To address this issue, we reconstruct matter sectors capable of restoring regularity while preserving the BTZ-like asymptotic structure. First, we derive regular black-hole solutions supported by nonlinear electrodynamics and determine the corresponding electromagnetic Lagrangians directly from the field equations. We then extend the analysis to Simpson--Visser black-bounce geometries, obtaining smooth throat configurations with finite curvature invariants throughout the spacetime. As an alternative regularization mechanism, we formulate a unimodular extension of lower-dimensional EGB gravity and show that standard Maxwell fields can support regular geometries through a dynamical exchange between the vacuum and matter sectors mediated by a spacetime-dependent cosmological function. We further investigate the thermodynamic properties of the regular black-hole and black-bounce solutions, showing that the matter sector modifies the evaporation process, allows for remnant formation, and produces nontrivial phase transitions. In the black-bounce case, the thermodynamic quantities smoothly recover the EGB-BTZ behavior in the appropriate limit. These results demonstrate that lower-dimensional EGB gravity provides a useful laboratory for exploring the interplay between higher-curvature corrections, regular compact objects, nonlinear electrodynamics, and unimodular gravity.

</details>

TL;DR: The paper constructs an affine-null characteristic formulation of the Einstein-Maxwell system with a charged complex scalar field in $n$-dimensional spherical symmetry, recasting the equations into a hierarchical radial system plus a scalar transport equation, enabling systematic study of charged scalar collapse and higher-dimensional black hole solutions.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to establish a well-posed characteristic initial-boundary value framework for studying gravitational collapse and black hole formation involving charged scalar fields in higher dimensions, where standard formulations face challenges due to coordinate singularities and constraint propagation. \n\n
动机在于建立一个适定的特征初边值问题框架，用于研究高维中涉及带电标量场的引力坍缩与黑洞形成，因为在高维情形下标准形式会因坐标奇点和约束传播而面临困难。affine-null方法可规避这些问题，并自然容纳渐近结构与视界结构。

Method: The authors introduce auxiliary variables to decompose the Einstein-Maxwell–charged scalar field equations under $n$-dimensional spherical symmetry into a hierarchical set of ordinary differential equations along radial null hypersurfaces, complemented by a first-order transport equation for the scalar field. They formulate and analyze the characteristic initial-boundary value problem for three configurations: asymptotic infinity, the vertex (origin), and finite null boundaries. Asymptotic quantities (e.g., mass, charge) and balance laws are derived from the hierarchy.
\n\n
作者引入辅助变量，在$n$维球对称下将Einstein-Maxwell–带电标量场方程分解为沿径向零超曲面的一组分层常微分方程，并辅以标量场的一阶输运方程。他们针对三种构型（渐近无穷远、顶点（原点）和有限零边界）建立了特征初边值问题，并从该分层结构中导出了渐近物理量（如质量、电荷）及守恒律。

Result: The hierarchical system correctly reproduces the scalar-free Reissner-Nordström-Tangherlini solutions—including both non-extremal and extremal branches—as a consistency check. The framework yields well-defined asymptotic charges, balance laws, and a robust setting for future numerical or analytical exploration of charged scalar hair and black hole solutions in $n$ dimensions.
\n\n
该分层系统作为自洽性检验，成功复现了无标量情形下的Reissner-Nordström-Tangherlini解（包括非极端和极端分支）。该框架给出了明确定义的渐近电荷、守恒律，并为未来在$n$维中对带电标量毛发及黑洞解进行数值或解析研究提供了稳健基础。

Conclusion: The affine-null characteristic formulation provides a powerful and systematic tool for analyzing charged scalar field dynamics coupled to gravity in higher dimensions, with built-in control over boundary conditions at infinity, the origin, and horizons. It opens avenues for constructing exact solutions and studying stability, uniqueness, and no-hair theorems in generalized settings.
\n\n
affine-null特征形式为分析高维中引力与带电标量场耦合的动力学提供了一个强大且系统的工具，能自然控制无穷远、原点和视界处的边界条件。该方法为构造精确解以及研究广义设定下的稳定性、唯一性和无毛定理开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We develop an affine-null characteristic formulation of the Einstein-Maxwell system coupled to a charged complex scalar field in $n$-dimensional spherical symmetry. By introducing suitable auxiliary variables, the main field equations are cast into a hierarchical system of radial hypersurface equations, supplemented by a transport equation for the scalar field. We discuss the associated characteristic initial-boundary value problem for asymptotic, vertex and null-boundary configurations, and derive the corresponding asymptotic quantities and balance laws. As a consistency check, we recover the scalar-free Reissner-Nordström-Tangherlini family, including both the non-extremal and extremal branches, directly from the hierarchy. The resulting framework provides a systematic setting for the study of charged scalar dynamics and exact black-hole solutions in higher-dimensional affine-null coordinates.

### [70] [Search for High-Frequency Gravitational Waves via Geomagnetic Conversion with Radio Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13642)
*Hongliang Tian,Lei Wu,Xiaolong Yang,Qiang Yuan,Bin Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper reports the first search for high-frequency gravitational waves (HFGWs) in the 1 GHz–1 THz band by detecting their conversion to electromagnetic radiation via the inverse Gertsenshtein effect in Earth's magnetic field, using radio telescopes (VLA and ALMA). No significant signal was found, leading to new upper limits on the characteristic strain $h_c \lesssim 10^{-18}$, improving prior constraints by up to three orders of magnitude.
\n\n
本文报道了首次利用地球磁场中逆Gertsenshtein效应将高频引力波（HFGWs）转换为电磁辐射，并通过射电望远镜（VLA和ALMA）在1 GHz–1 THz频段进行搜寻。未发现显著信号，由此得到特征应变的新上限 $h_c \\lesssim 10^{-18}$，比现有约束提高了最多三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: High-frequency gravitational waves (HFGWs) above 10 kHz are predicted by various models of exotic astrophysical sources and beyond-Standard-Model physics, yet remain largely unexplored due to the lack of suitable detection techniques. Traditional interferometric detectors like LIGO are insensitive at such frequencies. The inverse Gertsenshtein effect—where HFGWs convert into photons in the presence of a magnetic field—offers a promising alternative pathway for probing this uncharted regime.
\n\n
高于10 kHz的高频引力波（HFGWs）被多种奇异天体物理源及超出标准模型的新物理所预言，但由于缺乏合适的探测技术，该频段基本未被探索。传统干涉仪（如LIGO）在此频率范围不敏感。逆Gertsenshtein效应——即HFGWs在磁场中转化为光子——为此未知领域提供了一条有前景的探测路径。

Method: The authors exploit the inverse Gertsenshtein effect, wherein an incident HFGW traversing Earth's magnetic field generates a secondary electromagnetic wave. They analyze archival radio data from the Very Large Array (VLA) and Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA), searching for narrowband excess emission that would correspond to converted HFGW signals. By modeling the conversion efficiency and accounting for instrumental noise and background, they derive upper limits on the gravitational-wave characteristic strain $h_c(f)$ across the 1 GHz–1 THz frequency range.
\n\n
作者利用逆Gertsenshtein效应，即入射HFGW穿过地球磁场时产生次级电磁波。他们分析了甚大阵列（VLA）和阿塔卡马大型毫米波/亚毫米波阵列（ALMA）的存档射电数据，搜寻可能对应于转换后HFGW信号的窄带超额辐射。通过建模转换效率并考虑仪器噪声与背景，他们在1 GHz–1 THz频段内推导出引力波特征应变 $h_c(f)$ 的上限。

Result: No statistically significant electromagnetic signal attributable to HFGWs was detected. The study sets the most stringent upper limits to date on the characteristic strain in the 1 GHz–1 THz band, with $h_c \lesssim 10^{-18}$ at certain frequencies—improving existing constraints by up to three orders of magnitude. This represents the first direct observational constraint on HFGWs using ground-based radio telescopes via the inverse Gertsenshtein effect.
\n\n
未探测到可归因于HFGWs的具有统计显著性的电磁信号。该研究在1 GHz–1 THz频段设定了迄今为止最严格的特征应变上限，在某些频率处达到 $h_c \\lesssim 10^{-18}$，比现有约束提高了最多三个数量级。这是首次利用地面射电望远镜通过逆Gertsenshtein效应对HFGWs施加直接观测限制。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates the viability of using existing radio observatories to probe high-frequency gravitational waves through photon-graviton conversion in planetary magnetic fields. Although no detection was made, the significantly improved upper limits open a new observational window for exotic physics. Future instruments like the Square Kilometre Array (SKA) will enhance sensitivity and potentially enable the first detection of HFGWs via this method.
\n\n
本工作证明了利用现有射电观测设备通过行星磁场中的光子-引力子转换来探测高频引力波的可行性。尽管未实现探测，但显著改进的上限为探索奇异物理开辟了新的观测窗口。未来的设备如平方公里阵列（SKA）将进一步提升灵敏度，并可能首次通过此方法探测到HFGWs。

Abstract: The detection of high-frequency gravitational waves (HFGWs) above 10 kHz provides a crucial probe of exotic astrophysical phenomena and new physics. We report the first search for HFGWs via their conversion to electromagnetic radiation through the inverse Gertsenshtein effect in Earth's magnetic field, utilizing radio telescopes including the Very Large Array (VLA) and the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA). Since no statistically significant signal is observed, we obtain new upper limits on the characteristic strain across the 1 GHz -- 1 THz band, with the most stringent constraint reaching $h_c \lesssim 10^{-18}$, improving upon existing bounds by up to three orders of magnitude. These results significantly advance the exploration of uncharted parameter space for exotic gravitational-wave sources, paving the way for future discoveries with next-generation facilities such as the Square Kilometre Array (SKA).

</details>

### [71] [Observing Cosmic Reheating with the expanded Simons Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12519)
*Lei Ming,Marco Drewes*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: The Simons Observatory's 2027 upgrade with six Small Aperture Telescopes could, following a detection of primordial gravitational waves with $r = 0.0036$ or $r = 0.01$, constrain the reheating temperature and inflaton couplings in plateau inflation models; in QCD-driven Warm Inflation, these parameters might be measured to a few percent precision, enabling targeted axion experiments to test the hot Big Bang's initial conditions.
\n\n
Simons Observatory 将于 2027 年升级为六台 Small Aperture Telescopes，若探测到原初引力波（$r = 0.0036$ 或 $r = 0.01$），可在 plateau inflation 模型中限制 reheating temperature 和 inflaton 耦合；在 QCD-driven Warm Inflation 中，这些参数甚至可能以百分之几的精度被测量，从而指导 axion 实验检验热大爆炸初始条件的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A detection of primordial gravitational waves would fix the energy scale of inflation in many models, thereby opening a window to probe post-inflationary physics such as reheating dynamics and inflaton couplings. The paper aims to assess whether the upgraded Simons Observatory can provide meaningful constraints on these fundamental parameters, especially in well-motivated scenarios like plateau inflation and QCD-driven Warm Inflation.
\n\n
原初引力波的探测将固定许多模型中的 inflation 能标，从而打开探索 reheating 动力学和 inflaton 耦合等暴胀后物理的窗口。本文旨在评估升级后的 Simons Observatory 是否能在 plateau inflation 和 QCD-driven Warm Inflation 等有动机的场景中对这些基本参数提供有意义的限制。

Method: The authors assume benchmark detections of tensor-to-scalar ratio $r = 0.0036$ or $r = 0.01$ by the extended Simons Observatory configuration (six Small Aperture Telescopes by 2027). They then use theoretical relations between $r$, the inflationary potential, reheating temperature $T_{\\text{reh}}$, and inflaton couplings—particularly to gluons in QCD-driven Warm Inflation—to forecast parameter constraints. Optimistic instrumental and modeling assumptions are adopted to estimate achievable precision.
\n\n
作者假设升级后的 Simons Observatory（2027 年部署六台 Small Aperture Telescopes）能探测到基准值 $r = 0.0036$ 或 $r = 0.01$。随后利用 $r$、inflation 势、reheating temperature $T_{\\text{reh}}$ 以及 inflaton 耦合（尤其在 QCD-driven Warm Inflation 中与胶子的耦合）之间的理论关系，预测参数限制。采用乐观的仪器性能和模型假设以估计可达到的精度。

Result: In plateau inflation models, a detection of $r$ would determine the inflation scale and allow estimation of the order of magnitude of $T_{\\text{reh}}$ and inflaton interactions. In QCD-driven Warm Inflation, under optimistic assumptions, $T_{\\text{reh}}$ and the inflaton-gluon coupling could be measured with a few percent precision. This would yield concrete predictions for axion laboratory experiments searching for the inflaton.
\n\n
在 plateau inflation 模型中，$r$ 的探测将确定 inflation 能标，并允许估计 $T_{\\text{reh}}$ 和 inflaton 相互作用的大致量级。在 QCD-driven Warm Inflation 中，在乐观假设下，$T_{\\text{reh}}$ 和 inflaton-胶子耦合可被测量至百分之几的精度，从而为 axion 实验中寻找 inflaton 提供明确预言。

Conclusion: An r-detection by the upgraded Simons Observatory would not only confirm inflation but also enable indirect reconstruction of post-inflationary physics. Particularly in Warm Inflation scenarios, it could lead to quantitatively testable predictions for laboratory axion searches, bridging cosmological observations with particle physics experiments probing the origin of the hot Big Bang.
\n\n
升级后的 Simons Observatory 对 $r$ 的探测不仅将证实 inflation，还能间接重构暴胀后物理。尤其在 Warm Inflation 场景中，它可导出实验室 axion 搜索中可定量检验的预言，从而连接宇宙学观测与探索热大爆炸起源的粒子物理实验。

Abstract: The Simons Observatory will be extended by three Small Aperture Telescopes by 2027, increasing the total number of these instruments to six. We study the prospects for probing the reheating temperature and the inflaton coupling with this configuration, assuming a discovery of primordial gravitational waves in benchmark scenarios with a tensor-to-scalar ratio r=0.0036 or r=0.01. In popular plateau models of inflation, such an observation would fix the scale of inflation and enable determination of the order of magnitude of the reheating temperature and the inflaton interactions. For QCD-driven Warm Inflation the reheating temperature and inflaton coupling to gluons could, under optimistic assumptions, be measured with a precision of a few percent. Such a measurement would imply a clear prediction for complementary inflaton searches in axion experiments, paving the way toward probing the mechanism responsible for the initial conditions of the hot Big Bang in the laboratory.

### [72] [Dark Matter with a Drag at Low Redshift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12521)
*Martin Schmaltz,Eashwar N. Sivarajan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Recent $fσ_8$ and weak-lensing data suggest suppressed linear growth at $z \lesssim 1$ compared to $Λ$CDM predictions. The paper proposes an interacting Decaying Cold Dark Matter (iDCDM) model where dark matter decays into dark radiation at late times, generating a growing drag that suppresses structure growth. This two-parameter extension of $Λ$CDM preserves the background expansion, BBN, and primary CMB but predicts a step-like suppression in the scale- and redshift-dependent growth rate $f(k,z)$, testable by DESI, Euclid, and Rubin. Current data show a modest preference for iDCDM over $Λ$CDM ($Δχ^2 = -2.7$ to $-7.6$), primarily from $fσ_8$ measurements.

近期的 $fσ_8$ 和弱引力透镜数据显示，在 $z \lesssim 1$ 处线性增长率可能低于 $Λ$CDM 的预测。本文提出了一种相互作用衰变冷暗物质（interacting Decaying Cold Dark Matter, iDCDM）模型，其中暗物质在晚期衰变为暗辐射，从而产生随时间增强的拖曳效应，抑制结构增长。该模型在 $Λ$CDM 基础上仅引入两个新参数，同时保持背景演化、原初核合成（BBN）和主CMB不变，但预言了尺度和红移依赖的线性增长率 $f(k,z)$ 出现阶梯状压低，可被DESI、Euclid和Rubin观测检验。当前数据对iDCDM显示出适度偏好（$Δχ^2 = -2.7$ 到 $-7.6$），主要来自 $fσ_8$ 测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tensions between low-redshift large-scale structure observations (particularly $fσ_8$ and weak lensing) and $Λ$CDM predictions motivate exploring dark matter models that suppress structure growth at late times ($z \lesssim 1$). Conventional DM--DR interactions weaken with cosmic expansion, making them ineffective at late times; thus, a novel mechanism is needed to generate a growing interaction strength.
\n\n
低红移大尺度结构观测（尤其是 $fσ_8$ 和弱引力透镜）与 $Λ$CDM 预测之间的张力，促使人们探索能在晚期（$z \lesssim 1$）抑制结构增长的暗物质模型。传统的暗物质—暗辐射（DM–DR）相互作用随宇宙膨胀而减弱，因此在晚期无效；需要一种新机制来实现随时间增强的相互作用强度。

Method: The authors construct a particle physics model called interacting Decaying Cold Dark Matter (iDCDM), where cold dark matter particles decay at late times into dark radiation. This newly produced dark radiation then exerts a growing drag force on the remaining dark matter, slowing its clustering. The model introduces only two additional parameters beyond $Λ$CDM and is designed to leave the homogeneous background evolution, Big Bang Nucleosynthesis (BBN), and primary CMB anisotropies unaffected. The resulting modification manifests as a step-like suppression in the linear growth rate $f(k,z)$, which depends on both scale $k$ and redshift $z$.
\n\n
作者构建了一种名为相互作用衰变冷暗物质（interacting Decaying Cold Dark Matter, iDCDM）的粒子物理模型，其中冷暗物质粒子在晚期衰变为暗辐射。新产生的暗辐射对剩余暗物质施加一个随时间增强的拖曳力，减缓其成团。该模型在 $Λ$CDM 基础上仅引入两个额外参数，并确保均匀背景演化、大爆炸核合成（BBN）和主CMB各向异性不受影响。由此产生的修正表现为线性增长率 $f(k,z)$ 中的阶梯状压低，该压低依赖于尺度 $k$ 和红移 $z$。

Result: The iDCDM model yields a distinctive step-shaped suppression in $f(k,z)$, offering a clear observational signature for upcoming surveys like DESI, Euclid, and Rubin. When tested against current cosmological datasets, iDCDM shows a modest statistical preference over $Λ$CDM, with $Δχ^2$ ranging from $-2.7$ to $-7.6$, depending on assumptions about the redshift scaling of the drag force and the sum of neutrino masses. The improvement is primarily driven by better agreement with $fσ_8$ measurements.
