iDCDM 模型在 $f(k,z)$ 中产生独特的阶梯状压低，为DESI、Euclid和Rubin等即将开展的巡天提供了清晰的观测特征。在与当前宇宙学数据集对比时，iDCDM 相较于 $Λ$CDM 显示出适度的统计偏好，$Δχ^2$ 范围为 $-2.7$ 到 $-7.6$，具体取决于对拖曳力红移标度及中微子质量之和的假设。这一改进主要源于与 $fσ_8$ 测量结果的更好吻合。

Conclusion: The iDCDM model provides a theoretically motivated and observationally testable solution to the $S_8$/$fσ_8$ tension by introducing late-time DM decay into DR, generating a growing drag that suppresses late-time structure growth without altering early-universe physics. While current data mildly favor iDCDM, definitive confirmation or exclusion will require high-precision, $k$- and $z$-resolved measurements of the growth rate from next-generation large-scale structure surveys.
\n\n
iDCDM 模型通过引入暗物质在晚期衰变为暗辐射的机制，提供了一个理论上有动机且可观测检验的方案，以解决 $S_8$/$fσ_8$ 张力：该机制产生随时间增强的拖曳效应，抑制晚期结构增长，同时不改变早期宇宙物理。尽管当前数据对该模型仅有轻微偏好，但最终确认或排除将依赖于下一代大尺度结构巡天提供的高精度、$k$ 和 $z$ 分辨的增长率测量。

Abstract: Recent analyses of $fσ_8$ and weak-lensing data indicate that the linear growth rate at $z\lesssim 1$ may be lower than predicted by $Λ$CDM. This motivates models of dark matter in which large scale structure growth slows relative to $Λ$CDM at late times. We construct particle models in which dark matter experiences a drag with dark radiation that grows at late times, unlike conventional DM--DR interactions, which fade as the universe expands. A key ingredient is that the radiation interacting with the dark matter is produced at late times from dark matter decay. An explicit model, interacting Decaying Cold Dark Matter (iDCDM), adds two parameters beyond $Λ$CDM while leaving the background, BBN, and primary CMB intact. But it predicts a step-shaped suppression of the linear growth rate $f(k,z)$, a distinctive target for DESI, Euclid, and Rubin. Confronted with current data, iDCDM shows a modest preference over $Λ$CDM, driven by $fσ_8$, with $Δχ^2$ between $-2.7$ and $-7.6$ depending on the assumed scaling of the drag with redshift and on neutrino masses. The decisive test will come from upcoming $k$- and $z$-resolved growth measurements.

</details>


### [73] [Magnetic fields at the dawn of structure formation I. The CARLA J1510+5958 proto-cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12522)
*A. Pagliotta,A. Bonafede,C. Stuardi,C. J. Riseley,D. Vallés-Péerez,P. Tozzi,L. Di Mascolo*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper presents observational and simulation-based evidence for a magnetized intra-cluster medium (ICM) in the proto-cluster CARLA J1510+5958 at redshift $z = 1.72$. Using JVLA L-band polarimetry and Rotation Measure (RM) synthesis, the authors find asymmetric Faraday rotation between two AGN lobes, with the Western lobe showing ordered magnetic fields and the Eastern lobe depolarized. Combined with 3D MHD simulations, they infer a lower limit of 0.4 $μ$G for the magnetic field strength in the proto-ICM, demonstrating that magnetic fields are amplified during early cluster formation and likely influenced by AGN feedback.
\n\n
本文通过观测和模拟，提供了红移 $z = 1.72$ 处原星系团 CARLA J1510+5958 中存在磁化星系团内介质（ICM）的证据。作者利用 JVLA L 波段偏振观测和 Rotation Measure（RM）合成技术，发现两个 AGN 射电瓣之间存在不对称的法拉第旋转：西瓣显示有序磁场，东瓣则去偏振。结合 3D 磁流体动力学（MHD）模拟，推断出原 ICM 中磁场强度下限为 0.4 $μ$G，表明磁场在星系团早期形成阶段即已被放大，并可能受 AGN 反馈影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding the origin and evolution of cosmic magnetic fields remains a major open problem in astrophysics. While magnetic fields are well observed in local galaxy clusters, their presence, strength, and structure at high redshift—particularly in proto-clusters, the progenitors of today’s massive clusters—are poorly constrained. This work aims to probe the magnetisation of the intra-cluster medium during the early stages of cluster assembly, using the proto-cluster CARLA J1510+5958 as a laboratory to test whether magnetic fields are already amplified at $z \sim 1.7$ and how AGN activity contributes to this process.
\n\n
宇宙磁场的起源与演化仍是天体物理学中的重大未解问题。尽管本地星系团中的磁场已被充分观测，但在高红移——尤其是在原星系团（当今大质量星系团的前身）中，磁场的存在、强度和结构仍缺乏约束。本研究旨在探测星系团组装早期阶段 ICM 的磁化状态，以原星系团 CARLA J1510+5958 为实验室，检验在 $z \sim 1.7$ 时磁场是否已被放大，以及 AGN 活动如何参与这一过程。

Method: The authors analyze polarized radio emission from a central radio-loud AGN in the proto-cluster CARLA J1510+5958 using JVLA L-band (1–2 GHz) data. They apply Rotation Measure (RM) synthesis and QU-fitting techniques to extract Faraday rotation signatures from the two AGN lobes. To interpret the observed RM distributions, they perform 3D simulations of turbulent magnetic fields and gas density along different lines of sight, varying AGN orientation and path length through the proto-ICM. The simulations include both isotropic random fields and structured configurations to test compatibility with observations.
\n\n
作者利用 JVLA L 波段（1–2 GHz）数据，分析原星系团 CARLA J1510+5958 中心射电噪 AGN 的偏振辐射。他们采用 Rotation Measure（RM）合成和 QU 拟合技术，从两个 AGN 射电瓣中提取法拉第旋转信号。为解释观测到的 RM 分布，他们进行了 3D 模拟，包含湍流磁场和气体密度，并在不同视线方向上改变 AGN 取向和穿过原 ICM 的路径长度。模拟既包含各向同性随机磁场，也包含有序结构，以检验与观测结果的一致性。

Result: The Western AGN lobe shows a uniform RM ($-115 \pm 32\ \text{rad m}^{-2}$) with low dispersion ($36 \pm 11\ \text{rad m}^{-2}$), suggesting a locally ordered magnetic field likely compressed by the lobe. In contrast, the Eastern lobe is depolarized, indicating a more turbulent or complex magneto-ionic environment. Simulations rule out a purely isotropic random magnetic field for the Western lobe. QU fitting reveals an internal Faraday component, interpreted as mixing of magnetized relativistic plasma from the lobe with ambient proto-ICM gas. From the RM asymmetry and modeling, the authors derive a lower limit of 0.4 $μ$G for the volume-averaged magnetic field strength in the proto-ICM.
\n\n
西 AGN 射电瓣显示出均匀的 RM（$-115 \pm 32\ \text{rad m}^{-2}$），色散较低（$36 \pm 11\ \text{rad m}^{-2}$），表明存在局部有序磁场，可能由射电瓣压缩形成。相比之下，东瓣去偏振，表明其磁离子环境更湍动或复杂。模拟排除了西瓣 RM 分布源于纯各向同性随机磁场的可能性。QU 拟合揭示了一个内禀法拉第成分，被解释为射电瓣中的磁化相对论性等离子体与周围原 ICM 气体混合所致。基于 RM 不对称性和模型，作者得出原 ICM 中体积平均磁场强度的下限为 0.4 $μ$G。

Conclusion: The study provides direct evidence for a magnetized proto-intracluster medium at $z = 1.72$, confirming that magnetic fields are amplified during the early assembly of galaxy clusters. The observed RM asymmetry and internal Faraday component strongly suggest that AGN feedback plays a key role in magnetising the surrounding medium. These findings challenge models where magnetic fields are seeded only at late times and support scenarios of early amplification via turbulence and AGN-driven processes during cosmic structure formation.
\n\n
本研究为 $z = 1.72$ 处存在磁化的原星系团内介质提供了直接证据，证实磁场在星系团早期组装过程中已被放大。观测到的 RM 不对称性和内禀法拉第成分强烈表明 AGN 反馈在磁化周围介质中起关键作用。这些发现挑战了仅在晚期才播下磁场种子的模型，支持在宇宙结构形成早期通过湍流和 AGN 驱动过程实现磁场放大的情景。

Abstract: Magnetic fields are a fundamental ingredient of the Universe, influencing the formation and evolution of cosmic structures. While magnetic fields in local galaxy clusters have been studied, their origin, amplification, and strength at high redshift are poorly understood. Proto-clusters represent the early stages of galaxy cluster formation, ideal for investigating the early magnetisation of the intra-cluster medium (ICM). We present a study of CARLA J1510+5958 proto-cluster at z = 1.72, observed with the JVLA in the L-band (1-2 GHz). We aim to investigate the magnetic field strength and structure in the proto-ICM and the role of AGN in magnetising the environment during early cluster formation. We analyse Faraday rotation on the polarised emission from the central radio-loud AGN using the Rotation Measure (RM) synthesis and QU fitting technique. We further interpret the observations with 3D simulations of gas density and turbulent magnetic fields, varying AGN orientation and path length. The two AGN lobes show different polarisation properties. The Western lobe exhibits a uniform RM (average $-115 \pm 32\text{ rad m}^{-2}$, dispersion $36 \pm 11\text{ rad m}^{-2}$), indicating a locally ordered magnetic field likely compressed by the lobe, while the Eastern lobe is depolarised. Although the asymmetry indicates a turbulent, magnetised medium, simulations rule out a purely isotropic random field for the Western lobe RM distribution. The QU fitting further suggests an internal Faraday component, interpreted as magnetised relativistic plasma from the lobe mixed with the surrounding gas, indicating possible magnetisation of the ambient medium by the AGN. From this asymmetry, we constrain the average physical magnetic field in the proto-ICM to a lower limit of 0.4 $μ$G. These results confirm a magnetised proto-ICM at z = 1.72, proving early field amplification during cluster assembly.

</details>


### [74] [Complex yet Hermitian: Gaussian covariance of cross-correlation and multi-tracer power spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12551)
*Federico Montano,Stefano Camera,Emiliano Sefusatti,Mohamed Yousry Elkhashab*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper generalizes the theoretical framework for the Gaussian covariance of multi-tracer power spectrum estimators to include both even- and odd-parity (complex) power spectra, providing a unified formalism applicable to real-space and Fourier-space clustering statistics such as Legendre multipoles and 2D power spectra. The results are validated against Gaussian Monte Carlo simulations.
\n\n
本文将多示踪功率谱估计量的高斯协方差理论框架推广至包含偶宇称和奇宇称（复数）功率谱的情形，提供了一个适用于实空间和傅里叶空间聚类统计量（如Legendre多极矩和二维功率谱）的统一形式体系。结果通过高斯Monte Carlo模拟进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Future cosmological surveys will measure large-scale structure with high precision, necessitating accurate covariance modeling for clustering observables. Simulation-based covariance estimation is computationally expensive, especially for multi-tracer analyses and parity-odd signals in two-point statistics—techniques that reduce cosmic variance and probe relativistic effects. Existing analytical results for Gaussian covariance are limited to even-parity (real) power spectra, motivating a generalization to complex (parity-even and -odd) cases.
\n\n
未来的宇宙学巡天将以高精度测量大尺度结构，因此需要对聚类观测量进行精确的协方差建模。基于模拟的协方差估计计算成本高昂，尤其对于多示踪分析和两点统计中的奇宇称信号——这些技术可降低宇宙方差并探测相对论性效应。现有的高斯协方差解析结果仅限于偶宇称（实数）功率谱，因此有必要将其推广至复数（包含偶宇称和奇宇称）情形。

Method: The authors derive a general expression for the Gaussian covariance of a generic weighted multi-tracer power spectrum estimator, allowing for complex-valued spectra that encode both even- and odd-parity contributions. They first formulate the covariance for an arbitrary weighting scheme, then specialize it to two common cases: (1) Legendre multipoles $P_\\ell(k)$ obtained via angular decomposition, and (2) the two-dimensional power spectrum $P(k_\\parallel, k_\\perp)$. The derivation accounts for the full Hermitian structure of the complex power spectrum, including the imaginary part's antisymmetric properties. Predictions are compared against Gaussian random field Monte Carlo simulations.
\n\n
作者推导了一个通用加权多示踪功率谱估计量的高斯协方差的一般表达式，允许包含偶宇称和奇宇称贡献的复数值谱。他们首先针对任意加权方案构建协方差形式，然后将其具体应用于两种常见情形：(1) 通过角分解得到的Legendre多极矩 $P_\\ell(k)$，以及 (2) 二维功率谱 $P(k_\\parallel, k_\\perp)$。推导中考虑了复功率谱的完整Hermitian结构，包括其虚部的反对称性质。预测结果与高斯随机场Monte Carlo模拟进行了对比。

Result: The generalized covariance formula correctly reduces to known results in the limits of real (even-parity-only) power spectra and single-tracer analyses. For complex spectra, the covariance matrix exhibits a block structure where the imaginary part is purely antisymmetric (i.e., Hermitian), consistent with theoretical expectations. Monte Carlo simulations confirm the accuracy of the analytical predictions across various configurations, validating the treatment of parity-odd contributions and cross-tracer terms.
\n\n
该广义协方差公式在实数（仅偶宇称）功率谱和单示踪分析的极限下正确还原为已知结果。对于复数谱，协方差矩阵呈现出块状结构，其中虚部是纯反对称的（即满足Hermitian性质），符合理论预期。Monte Carlo模拟在多种构型下证实了解析预测的准确性，验证了对奇宇称贡献和示踪交叉项的处理。

Conclusion: The work provides a robust, general analytical framework for computing the Gaussian covariance of multi-tracer power spectrum measurements that include parity-odd signals. This formalism enables efficient and accurate covariance estimation for next-generation cosmological surveys aiming to exploit relativistic and wide-angle effects through complex power spectrum estimators, without relying on costly simulations.
\n\n
本工作为包含奇宇称信号的多示踪功率谱测量提供了一个稳健且通用的高斯协方差解析框架。该形式体系使得下一代宇宙学巡天能够高效、准确地估计协方差，从而通过复功率谱估计量探测相对论性和宽角效应，而无需依赖昂贵的模拟。

Abstract: Accurate modelling of the covariance of clustering observables is essential to fully exploit current and future survey data, which is expected to constrain large-scale clustering signals with unprecedented precision. Computational costs of simulation-based estimates motivate analytical approaches, especially in light of the growing interest towards multi-tracer analyses and parity-odd signatures in two-point statistics, which respectively mitigate cosmic variance and probe relativistic projection effects on cosmological scales. In this work, we generalise previous theoretical results for the Gaussian covariance of multi-tracer power spectrum measurements, providing a general expression applicable to both real (even-parity) and complex (both even- and odd-parity) power spectra. We focus on a generic weighted estimator at first, and then showcase how our general formalism applies to Legendre power spectrum multipoles and two-dimensional power spectrum, recovering known limits in appropriate cases. We validate our predictions against Gaussian Monte Carlo simulations and investigate the structure of the covariance matrix, including the Hermitian properties of its imaginary part.

</details>


### [75] [Galaxy formation in modified gravity -- II. galaxy halo connection and assembly bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12565)
*Michael Collier,Sownak Bose,Baojiu Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Modern Stage-IV galaxy surveys like DESI and Euclid aim to constrain cosmology and test modified gravity (MG), but are limited by uncertainties in the galaxy-halo connection—especially due to galaxy bias and assembly bias (AB). Using mock ELG and LRG catalogs from $f(R)$ MG hydrodynamical simulations, the paper shows that standard halo occupation distribution (HOD) models ignoring AB underestimate clustering by 10–20% in $Λ$CDM at $z\lesssim1$. Including environment density as a secondary HOD parameter reduces AB-induced errors to 2–3% for $z\lesssim0.5$, offering a robust framework for testing MG with large-scale structure (LSS).
\n\n
现代Stage-IV星系巡天（如DESI和Euclid）旨在限制宇宙学参数并检验modified gravity (MG)，但受限于星系-晕关联（galaxy-halo connection）中的不确定性——尤其是星系bias和assembly bias (AB)。本文利用来自$f(R)$ MG流体动力学模拟的ELG和LRG模拟星表，表明忽略AB的标准halo occupation distribution (HOD)模型在$z\lesssim1$时会低估$Λ$CDM中的星系成团性达10–20%。将环境密度作为HOD的第二参数可将所有模型中AB效应降至$z\lesssim0.5$时的2–3%，为利用large-scale structure (LSS)检验非标准宇宙学模型提供了稳健起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The primary motivation is to address the limitations imposed by poorly understood galaxy-halo connection physics—particularly galaxy bias and assembly bias—in the context of testing modified gravity (MG) models with next-generation large-scale structure (LSS) data. While MG models like $f(R)$ gravity offer alternatives to $Λ$CDM for explaining cosmic acceleration, their signatures in galaxy clustering can be degenerate with or obscured by complex baryonic and halo assembly effects. Current HOD frameworks, which assume galaxy occupation depends only on halo mass, fail to capture these nuances, especially in MG scenarios involving screening mechanisms such as the chameleon effect.
\n\n
主要动机是解决在利用下一代large-scale structure (LSS)数据检验modified gravity (MG)模型时，由星系-晕关联物理（特别是星系bias和assembly bias）理解不足所带来的限制。尽管$f(R)$ gravity等MG模型为解释宇宙加速膨胀提供了$Λ$CDM之外的替代方案，但其在星系成团性中的信号可能与复杂的重子物理及晕组装效应简并或被掩盖。当前的halo occupation distribution (HOD)框架假设星系占据仅依赖于晕质量，无法捕捉这些细微效应，尤其在涉及chameleon screening mechanism等屏蔽机制的MG情形中。

Method: The authors employ state-of-the-art hydrodynamical simulations of $f(R)$ modified gravity to generate realistic mock catalogs of emission line galaxies (ELGs) and luminous red galaxies (LRGs) that match the specifications of DESI and Euclid. They analyze the galaxy-halo connection by comparing clustering predictions from standard HOD models (mass-only) against those from extended HOD models that incorporate local environment density as a secondary parameter. The environment is defined via the smoothed matter density field around halos. They quantify the impact of assembly bias (AB) by measuring deviations in two-point correlation functions between models with and without environmental dependence, across redshifts $z \lesssim 1$.
\n\n
作者利用先进的$f(R)$ modified gravity流体动力学模拟，生成符合DESI和Euclid巡天规格的emission line galaxies (ELGs)和luminous red galaxies (LRGs)的逼真模拟星表。他们通过比较标准HOD模型（仅依赖晕质量）与扩展HOD模型（引入局部环境密度作为第二参数）的成团性预测，分析星系-晕关联。环境密度通过晕周围平滑后的物质密度场定义。他们通过测量在红移$z \lesssim 1$范围内、有无环境依赖的模型之间两点相关函数的偏差，量化assembly bias (AB)的影响。

Result: In $Λ$CDM, neglecting assembly bias leads to a 10–20% underestimation of galaxy clustering amplitude at $z \lesssim 1$ when using standard mass-only HOD. In $f(R)$ MG models, the chameleon screening mechanism modifies halo and galaxy formation in a mass- and time-dependent way, exacerbating AB effects. However, augmenting the HOD with environment density as a second parameter reduces AB-induced clustering errors to just 2–3% for $z \lesssim 0.5$ across both $Λ$CDM and MG scenarios. This suggests that environmental dependence captures the dominant physical driver of AB in these models.
\n\n
在$Λ$CDM中，使用标准仅质量依赖的HOD忽略assembly bias会导致在$z \lesssim 1$时星系成团性幅度被低估10–20%。在$f(R)$ MG模型中，chameleon screening mechanism以质量和时间依赖的方式改变晕和星系的形成，加剧了AB效应。然而，在HOD中加入环境密度作为第二参数后，可在$z \lesssim 0.5$时将AB引起的成团性误差降至仅2–3%（适用于$Λ$CDM和MG两种情形）。这表明环境依赖性捕捉了这些模型中AB的主要物理驱动因素。

Conclusion: A minimal extension of the standard HOD framework—by including local environment density as a secondary occupation variable—effectively mitigates assembly bias down to the few-percent level for low-redshift LSS analyses. This provides a physically motivated and computationally tractable approach to model galaxy clustering in both $Λ$CDM and modified gravity cosmologies, enabling more robust tests of fundamental physics with Stage-IV survey data. Future work should refine the environmental proxy and validate this approach across a broader class of MG models.
\n\n
对标准HOD框架进行最小扩展——将局部环境密度作为第二占据变量——可有效将低红移LSS分析中的assembly bias抑制到几个百分点水平。这为在$Λ$CDM和modified gravity宇宙学中建模星系成团性提供了一个物理动机明确且计算可行的方法，从而能更稳健地利用Stage-IV巡天数据检验基础物理。未来工作应优化环境代理量，并在更广泛的MG模型中验证该方法。

Abstract: Modern surveys such as DESI and \textit{Euclid}, which collect data for hundreds of millions of galaxies to map the large-scale structure (LSS) of the Universe, hold the key to determining the cosmological parameters and testing new physics. This ambition, however, is limited by uncertainties in the galaxy-halo connection: the link between observed galaxies and the underlying, unobservable matter field, by accounting for effects such as galaxy bias and assembly bias (AB). These are particularly poorly-understood for modified gravity (MG) models, which are popular alternatives to the cosmological constant to explain accelerated expansion. We approach this problem using mock emission line galaxy (ELG) and luminous red galaxy (LRG) catalogues in $f(R)$ gravity matching the specifications of ongoing Stage-IV galaxy surveys, generated from state-of-the-art MG hydrodynamical simulations. While the interplay between MG -- especially the chameleon screening mechanism -- and galaxy formation leaves complicated imprints in the galaxy-halo connection, a simple physical picture emerges in which halo and galaxy formation are enhanced for progressively more massive haloes over time. We confirm that the basic galaxy-halo connection model, the halo occupation distribution (HOD), in which galaxy occupation is determined solely by halo mass, underestimates galaxy clustering strength in $Λ$CDM by $10$--$20\%$ at $z\lesssim1$ when neglecting AB, and demonstrate that MG introduces further complexity. Extending this model with a suitably-chosen environment density as a secondary HOD variable reduces the AB effect in all models to $2$--$3\%$ for $z\lesssim0.5$. This provides a well-motivated starting point for further works on minimising the impact of AB when testing non-standard cosmological models with LSS.

</details>


### [76] [Weak Lensing Spectrotomography: A1767 and A2065](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12613)
*Jubee Sohn,Ian P. Dell'Antonio,Margaret J. Geller*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper presents the first spectroscopic tomographic weak lensing (spectrotomography) measurements for galaxy clusters A1767 and A2065 using archival Subaru/HSC imaging combined with new spectroscopic redshifts, achieving $3.1σ$ and $3.5σ$ detections respectively. The method avoids photometric redshift systematics by using only spectroscopically confirmed background galaxies, yielding shear signals consistent with caustic mass estimates. Combined with a prior detection of A2029, these results demonstrate spectrotomography’s potential for unbiased cluster mass measurements and future geometric cosmological tests with Euclid and LSST.
\n\n
本文首次利用存档的Subaru/HSC成像数据与新的光谱红移，对星系团A1767和A2065进行了光谱断层弱引力透镜（spectrotomography）测量，分别达到$3.1σ$和$3.5σ$的探测显著性。该方法仅使用光谱确认的背景星系，避免了光度红移引入的系统误差，所得剪切信号与caustic质量估计一致。结合此前对A2029的探测，这些结果展示了spectrotomography在无偏星系团质量测定及未来利用Euclid和LSST进行几何宇宙学检验方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to develop and validate a robust weak lensing methodology—spectrotomography—that eliminates systematic uncertainties associated with photometric redshifts in background galaxy selection. By relying exclusively on spectroscopic redshifts, the technique cleanly separates cluster members from true background sources, enabling more accurate and unbiased mass estimates for galaxy clusters, which are crucial for cosmological inference.
\n\n
动机在于发展并验证一种稳健的弱引力透镜方法——spectrotomography，以消除在背景星系选择中由光度红移带来的系统不确定性。通过完全依赖光谱红移，该技术能清晰区分星系团成员与真实背景源，从而获得更准确、无偏的星系团质量估计，这对宇宙学推断至关重要。

Method: The authors perform spectroscopic tomographic weak lensing (spectrotomography) using extensive new spectroscopy to assign precise redshifts to galaxies around clusters A1767 and A2065, combined with deep imaging from Subaru/Hyper Suprime-Cam (HSC). Only galaxies with secure spectroscopic redshifts are used to define background source samples in multiple redshift bins, enabling tomographic shear measurements without reliance on photometric redshifts. The resulting shear profiles are compared to dynamical (caustic) mass estimates and examined for consistency with redshift scaling.
\n\n
作者利用围绕A1767和A2065星系团的新光谱数据为星系分配精确红移，并结合Subaru/Hyper Suprime-Cam（HSC）的深度成像，开展光谱断层弱引力透镜（spectrotomography）分析。仅使用具有可靠光谱红移的星系来定义多个红移区间中的背景源样本，从而在不依赖光度红移的情况下实现断层剪切测量。所得剪切轮廓与动力学（caustic）质量估计进行比较，并检验其是否符合红移缩放关系。

Result: Tomographic shear signals are detected at $3.1σ$ for A1767 and $3.5σ$ for A2065. The amplitudes agree with caustic mass estimates and exhibit the expected dependence on source redshift. However, comparison with the earlier DECam-based spectrophotometric detection of A2029 reveals potential systematics affecting bright background galaxies, possibly due to blending or calibration issues in HSC data. These effects warrant careful investigation for future large-scale applications.
\n\n
在A1767和A2065中分别探测到$3.1σ$和$3.5σ$的断层剪切信号。其幅度与caustic质量估计一致，并表现出预期的源红移依赖性。然而，与早期基于DECam的A2029光谱测光探测结果对比，揭示了影响较亮背景星系的潜在系统误差，可能源于HSC数据中的天体混合（blending）或定标问题。这些效应在未来大规模应用中需仔细研究。

Conclusion: Spectrotomography using spectroscopic redshifts provides a promising path toward unbiased weak lensing mass measurements for galaxy clusters. With three successful detections (A1767, A2029, A2065), the technique is now poised for broader application with upcoming surveys like Euclid and LSST, potentially enabling a novel geometric test of cosmology independent of traditional probes such as CMB or BAO.
\n\n
基于光谱红移的spectrotomography为实现无偏的星系团弱引力透镜质量测量提供了可行路径。随着三个成功探测案例（A1767、A2029、A2065）的积累，该技术已准备好在Euclid和LSST等未来巡天中广泛应用，有望实现一种独立于CMB或BAO等传统探针的新型几何宇宙学检验。

Abstract: We describe spectroscopic tomographic weak lensing measurements (spectrotomography) for two rich clusters of galaxies, A1767 and A2065, based on extensive spectroscopy and Subaru/Hyper Suprime-Cam (HSC) imaging. These detections represent the first use of spectrotomography based on archival Subaru/HSC imaging. The measurements depend only on galaxies with spectroscopic redshifts reported here. The approach cleanly separates cluster members from the background and suppresses systematics that may be introduced by the use of photometric background redshifts. We detect the tomographic shear signals at $3.1σ$ (A1767) and $3.5σ$ (A2065). The shear signal amplitudes are consistent with the cluster dynamical (caustic) masses and they scale appropriately with source redshift. However, comparison with the first spectrophotometric detection of A2029 based on DECam imaging reveals some subtle potential systematic issues in deriving the shear signal for the relatively bright background galaxies used in the analysis. These issues may be important for understanding future more extensive applications of spectrotomography based on further Subaru imaging, as well as Euclid and LSST data. The total of three spectrophotometric detections (A1767, A2029, and A2065) sets the stage for broader application of the technique for unbiased cluster weak lensing mass determinations and potentially for a geometric cosmological test that is independent of other methods.

</details>


### [77] [Identification of Lensed Gravitational-Wave Beat Patterns by LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12792)
*Hengyu Wu,Tonghua Liu,Kai Liao*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper investigates beat patterns in overlapping gravitational-wave images from strongly lensed massive black hole binary (MBHB) systems detectable by LISA. Using a singular isothermal sphere (SIS) lens model and waveform mismatch analysis during the inspiral phase, the authors estimate that ~7% of detectable two-image lensed events produce identifiable beat signals. Bayesian inference demonstrates that lensing parameters like time delay and magnification can be accurately recovered using a dedicated beat template.
\n\n
本文研究了LISA可探测的强引力透镜大质量黑洞双星（MBHB）系统的重叠引力波图像中产生的拍频模式。作者采用奇异等温球（SIS）透镜模型，并在旋近阶段通过波形失配分析，估计约7%的可探测双像透镜事件会产生可识别的拍频信号。贝叶斯推断表明，使用专门的拍频模板可以准确恢复时间延迟和放大率等透镜参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The long signal duration of LISA-band massive black hole binaries (months to years) enables multiple lensed images—generated by strong gravitational lensing—to overlap in time. This temporal overlap produces interference-like beat patterns in the observed waveform, offering a novel observational signature of strong lensing that differs from traditional methods relying on repeated merger events or image separation. The paper aims to quantify how often such beat patterns occur and whether they can be used to extract lensing parameters.
\n\n
LISA波段大质量黑洞双星信号持续时间长（数月至数年），使得强引力透镜产生的多个透镜图像可能在时间上重叠。这种时间重叠会在观测波形中产生类似干涉的拍频模式，为强透镜效应提供一种不同于传统方法（如重复并合事件或图像分离）的新颖观测特征。本文旨在量化此类拍频模式的发生频率，并评估其是否可用于提取透镜参数。

Method: The authors adopt a singular isothermal sphere (SIS) lens model to compute time delays and magnifications for two-image configurations. They construct beat waveforms by superposing two lensed inspiral signals with different arrival times and magnifications. To isolate the beat effect, they restrict waveform mismatch calculations to the overlapping inspiral stage before the first image’s coalescence, excluding the second image’s delayed merger peak. Using the HS-nod-SN (B+20) strong-lensing population simulation, they identify events satisfying both temporal overlap and beat-identification criteria. Bayesian parameter estimation is performed with a custom beat template to infer lensing parameters (time delay $\\Delta t$ and magnification ratio $\\mu$).
\n\n
作者采用奇异等温球（SIS）透镜模型计算双像构型的时间延迟和放大率。他们通过叠加两个具有不同到达时间和放大率的透镜旋近信号来构建拍频波形。为隔离拍频效应，他们将波形失配计算限制在第一个图像并合前的重叠旋近阶段，排除第二个图像延迟的并合峰贡献。利用HS-nod-SN（B+20）强透镜种群模拟，识别出同时满足时间重叠和拍频识别标准的事件。使用定制的拍频模板进行贝叶斯参数估计，以推断透镜参数（时间延迟 $\\Delta t$ 和放大率比 $\\mu$）。

Result: Among 196 detectable two-image lensed MBHB events in the simulated population, 92 exhibit temporal overlap during inspiral, and 14 meet the beat-identification criterion based on waveform mismatch thresholds, yielding an identifiable beat fraction of ~7%. For a representative beat event, posterior distributions from Bayesian inference using the beat template accurately recover the input lensing time delay and magnification, demonstrating the feasibility of parameter extraction from beat patterns.
\n\n
在模拟种群的196个可探测双像透镜MBHB事件中，92个在旋近阶段表现出时间重叠，其中14个满足基于波形失配阈值的拍频识别标准，可识别拍频事件占比约为7%。对于一个代表性拍频事件，使用拍频模板进行贝叶斯推断所得的后验分布能准确恢复输入的透镜时间延迟和放大率，证明了从拍频模式中提取参数的可行性。

Conclusion: Lensed beat patterns represent a distinct and identifiable subclass of strongly lensed LISA events. Their unique waveform morphology enables direct measurement of strong lensing parameters such as time delay and magnification without requiring observation of multiple merger peaks. This opens a new channel for studying strong gravitational lensing in the mHz gravitational-wave band and enhances LISA’s capability to probe cosmological structure and black hole populations.
\n\n
透镜拍频模式代表了一类独特且可识别的强透镜LISA事件子集。其独特的波形形态使得无需观测多个并合峰即可直接测量时间延迟和放大率等强透镜参数。这为在毫赫兹引力波波段研究强引力透镜开辟了新途径，并增强了LISA探测宇宙结构和黑洞种群的能力。

Abstract: Strong lensing of massive black hole binaries can produce multiple gravitational-wave images with different magnifications and arrival times. LISA signals remain in band for months to years, allowing multiple lensed images to overlap during the inspiral stage and generate beat patterns. A singular isothermal sphere lens model is adopted to describe the lensing configuration, and two-image beat waveforms are constructed from massive black hole binary signals. To isolate the beat pattern itself, waveform mismatch is evaluated only during the overlapping inspiral stage before the coalescence of the first image, excluding contributions from the delayed merger peak of the second image. Using the HS-nod-SN (B+20) strong-lensing population, the occurrence rate of identifiable beat events is estimated, and Bayesian parameter estimation is performed with a beat template. Beat patterns are most readily identified when the lensing time delay is short and the delayed image has a relatively large magnification. Among 196 detectable two-image lensed events, 92 satisfy the temporal-overlap condition and 14 satisfy the beat-identification criterion, corresponding to an identifiable beat fraction of about 7\%. Posterior inference shows that the beat template can recover the lensing time delay and magnification parameters for a representative beat event. These results indicate that lensed beat patterns constitute a distinguishable subset of strongly lensed LISA events and provide a unique observational signature of strong lensing in the LISA band.

</details>


### [78] [Geometric obstruction to resolving the Hubble tension: orthogonality of scale and shape in distance measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12822)
*Zhihuan Zhou,Zhuang Miao,Sheng Bi,Chaoqian Ai,Hongchao Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: The paper demonstrates that combining early-time sound-horizon reduction (via $α$) with flexible late-time dark energy ($w(z)$) cannot fully resolve the Hubble tension due to a geometric obstruction: BAO and SN Ia data require mutually incompatible $w(z)$ deformations (phantom vs. quintessence), quantified by $\cosθ = -0.97$. Even after optimizing $α = 0.992$, the joint fit yields $H_0 = 70.3 \pm 0.3$, still $3.2σ$ below SH0ES, revealing an irreducible disagreement between probes rooted in their independent constraints on $Ω_m$ and the inability of $w(z)$ to rescale absolute distances.
\n\n
本文表明，结合早期声视界缩减（通过参数 $α$）与灵活的晚期暗能量状态方程 $w(z)$ 无法完全解决哈勃张力，其原因是一种几何障碍：BAO 和超新星（SN Ia）数据要求互不相容的 $w(z)$ 变形（幻影暗能量 $w < -1$ 对比精质暗能量 $w > -1$），其反向对齐程度由 $\cosθ = -0.97$ 量化。即使优化后取 $α = 0.992$，联合拟合仍给出 $H_0 = 70.3 \pm 0.3$，比 SH0ES 结果低 $3.2σ$，揭示了不同探针之间在 $Ω_m$ 上的独立约束存在不可消除的矛盾，且 $w(z)$ 虽可重塑但无法重新标定绝对距离尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to understand whether the Hubble tension can be resolved within extensions of $Λ$CDM that simultaneously modify the early-universe sound horizon $r_s$ and allow arbitrary late-time smooth dark energy evolution $w(z)$. The authors aim to determine if the persistent discrepancy stems from insufficient model flexibility or from a fundamental incompatibility between cosmological probes.
\n\n
本文的动机在于探究哈勃张力是否能在同时修改早期宇宙声视界 $r_s$ 并允许任意晚期平滑暗能量演化 $w(z)$ 的 $Λ$CDM 扩展模型中得到解决。作者旨在判断这一持续存在的差异是源于模型自由度不足，还是源于不同宇宙学探针之间的根本性不兼容。

Method: The authors perform a full MCMC analysis using DESI DR2 BAO, Planck plik\_lite CMB, and Pantheon+ SN Ia data within a generalized framework parameterized by the sound-horizon rescaling $α = r_s^{\rm mod}/r_s^{Λ{\rm CDM}}$, a damping parameter $β_{\rm damp}$, and a flexible redshift-dependent dark energy equation of state $w(z)$. They analyze the geometry of the deformation space spanned by these parameters, particularly the response directions for $Ω_m$, and quantify the alignment (via $\cosθ$) between the $w(z)$ deformations required by BAO and SN datasets to reconcile $Ω_m$.
\n\n
作者在一个广义框架下，使用 DESI DR2 BAO、Planck plik\_lite CMB 和 Pantheon+ SN Ia 数据进行完整的 MCMC 分析，该框架由声视界重标度参数 $α = r_s^{\rm mod}/r_s^{Λ{\rm CDM}}$、阻尼参数 $β_{\rm damp}$ 以及灵活的红移依赖暗能量状态方程 $w(z)$ 参数化。他们分析了这些参数所张成的变形空间的几何结构，特别是对 $Ω_m$ 的响应方向，并通过 $\cosθ$ 量化了 BAO 与 SN 数据集为调和 $Ω_m$ 所需的 $w(z)$ 变形之间的对齐程度。

Result: The analysis reveals that the BAO--SN matter-density gap $ΔΩ_m = 0.037$ is invariant under $α$-rescaling within $Λ$CDM, and late-time $w(z)$ modifications cannot close this gap because they demand opposite behaviors: phantom-like ($w < -1$) for BAO and quintessence-like ($w > -1$) for SN at low redshift, with $\cosθ = -0.97$. The optimal fit gives $α^* = 0.992$, yielding $H_0 = 70.3 \pm 0.3\;\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, which remains $3.2σ$ below the SH0ES value. The deformation space already captures $93\%$ of the relevant $Ω_m$ response direction, yet the tension persists due to independent and conflicting $Ω_m$ constraints.
\n\n
分析表明，在 $Λ$CDM 框架内，BAO 与 SN 之间的物质密度间隙 $ΔΩ_m = 0.037$ 在 $α$ 重标度下严格不变，而晚期 $w(z)$ 修改无法弥合此间隙，因其对 BAO 要求幻影行为（$w < -1$），对低红移 SN 却要求精质行为（$w > -1$），二者反向对齐程度达 $\cosθ = -0.97$。最优拟合给出 $α^* = 0.992$，对应 $H_0 = 70.3 \pm 0.3\;\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$，仍比 SH0ES 值低 $3.2σ$。尽管变形空间已覆盖 $93\%$ 的相关 $Ω_m$ 响应方向，但由于不同探针对 $Ω_m$ 的独立且冲突的约束，张力依然存在。

Conclusion: The Hubble tension cannot be resolved by jointly adjusting the sound horizon and late-time dark energy, not due to lack of model freedom, but because of an intrinsic geometric obstruction: BAO and SN Ia probe $Ω_m$ through independent physical channels and yield irreconcilable requirements on $w(z)$. Moreover, the residual $H_0$ deficit is tied to the absolute distance scale set by the local distance ladder, which $w(z)$ can distort but not globally rescale. This implies the tension reflects a fundamental disagreement between early- and late-universe probes beyond current phenomenological extensions of $Λ$CDM.
\n\n
哈勃张力无法通过同时调整声视界和晚期暗能量来解决，其根源并非模型自由度不足，而是一种内在的几何障碍：BAO 与 SN Ia 通过独立的物理通道探测 $Ω_m$，并对 $w(z)$ 提出不可调和的要求。此外，剩余的 $H_0$ 缺口与本地距离阶梯设定的绝对距离尺度相关，而 $w(z)$ 只能扭曲却无法全局重标度该尺度。这表明张力反映了早期与晚期宇宙探针之间在当前 $Λ$CDM 现象学扩展之外的根本性分歧。

Abstract: We identify a geometric obstruction to resolving the Hubble tension by combining early-time sound-horizon reduction with late-time smooth dark energy. Within $Λ$CDM, the BAO--SN matter-density gap $ΔΩ_m = 0.037$ is exactly invariant under the sound-horizon rescaling $α\equiv r_s^{\rm mod}/r_s^{Λ{\rm CDM}}$, and late-time $w(z)$ deformations cannot eliminate this gap either: reconciling the two datasets requires \emph{opposite} deformations -- phantom ($w < -1$) for BAO, quintessence ($w > -1$) for SN at $z < 0.5$ -- an anti-alignment quantified by $\cosθ= -0.97$ in $w(z)$ space. A full MCMC analysis of DESI DR2 BAO, Planck plik\_lite, and Pantheon+ bears this out: the optimal $α^* = 0.992$ ($0.8\%$ $r_s$ reduction) brings the joint fit to $H_0 = 70.3 \pm 0.3\;\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$, still $3.2σ$ below SH0ES, with the inter-dataset tension reduced but not removed. The obstruction reflects not a shortage of model freedom but an irreducible disagreement between probes. The deformation space $\{α, β_{\rm damp}, w(z)\}$ already spans $93\%$ of the $Ω_m$ response direction; nonetheless BAO and SN constrain $Ω_m$ through independent channels and disagree, while the residual $H_0$ deficit, anchored by the local distance ladder, resides in the absolute distance scale that $w(z)$ reshapes but cannot rescale.

Abstract: The masses of galaxy clusters carry cosmological and astrophysical information. We develop a simulation-based inference pipeline to infer cluster masses from full projected phase-space information of member and interloper galaxies. Our method combines a permutation-invariant Deep Sets architecture with neural posterior estimation using normalizing flows, enabling the recovery of expressive posterior distributions. We train the model to predict residual corrections to the classical $M$--$σ$ relation, thus explicitly isolating information beyond velocity dispersion. Using the Uchuu-UniverseMachine simulation, we evaluate the method under both idealized (interloper-free) and realistic (cylindrical) observational setups. In the idealized case, our model reduces the scatter in mass estimates to as low as $\sim 0.1$ dex, representing a twofold improvement over the traditional $M$--$σ$ relation. In the cylindrical setup, we achieve comparable performance at the high-mass end ($> 10^{14.5}\,M_\odot/h$), demonstrating robustness against interloper contamination. We demonstrate that set-based simulation-driven inference provides a powerful and flexible framework for galaxy cluster mass estimation, enabling improved accuracy and reliable uncertainty characterization for upcoming large-scale surveys. Our model saturates the kinematic information content and thus suggests a baseline for future studies.

TL;DR: The Hubble tension—the discrepancy in $H_0$ measurements between early- and late-universe probes—persists even when using DESI BAO and uncalibrated Type Ia supernovae data, suggesting that the tension arises from new low-redshift physics rather than observational systematics.
\n\n
哈勃张力（即早期宇宙与晚期宇宙对 $H_0$ 测量值之间的差异）即使在使用 DESI BAO 与未校准的 Ia 型超新星数据时依然存在，表明该张力源于新的低红移物理机制，而非观测系统误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the growing inconsistency between the Hubble constant $H_0$ inferred from cosmic microwave background (CMB) data within the $Λ$CDM model and that measured from local distance-ladder methods. Recent data from DESI BAO combined with uncalibrated supernovae further exacerbate this tension without invoking local calibrations, pointing toward a potential breakdown of $Λ$CDM at low redshifts.
\n\n
动机源于在 $Λ$CDM 模型下由宇宙微波背景（CMB）推断出的哈勃常数 $H_0$ 与局域距离阶梯方法测量值之间日益加剧的不一致性。近期 DESI BAO 与未校准超新星数据的结合在不依赖局域校准的情况下进一步加剧了这一张力，暗示 $Λ$CDM 模型可能在低红移处失效。

Method: The paper reviews recent observational analyses, particularly focusing on the combination of Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (BAO) measurements with uncalibrated Type Ia supernovae data. This approach avoids reliance on the local distance ladder and tests whether the Hubble tension persists under such assumptions within or beyond $Λ$CDM.
\n\n
本文综述了近期的观测分析，特别聚焦于将暗能量光谱仪（DESI）重子声学振荡（BAO）测量与未校准的 Ia 型超新星数据相结合的方法。该方法避免依赖局域距离阶梯，并检验在 $Λ$CDM 或其扩展模型下哈勃张力是否仍然存在。

Result: The combination of DESI BAO and uncalibrated Type Ia supernovae yields a higher $H_0$ value that significantly deviates from the $Λ$CDM prediction based on early-universe probes. This indicates that the Hubble tension is not solely due to systematics in local measurements but likely reflects new physics operating at low redshifts.
\n\n
DESI BAO 与未校准 Ia 型超新星数据的结合给出了一个显著高于基于早期宇宙探针的 $Λ$CDM 预测的 $H_0$ 值。这表明哈勃张力并非仅由局域测量中的系统误差引起，而很可能反映了在低红移处起作用的新物理机制。

Conclusion: Current observational systematics are insufficient to explain the magnitude of the Hubble tension. The persistent discrepancy across multiple independent probes strongly suggests the need for new physical mechanisms beyond the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology, particularly those affecting the late-time expansion history of the universe.
\n\n
当前观测中的系统误差不足以解释哈勃张力的幅度。多种独立探针之间持续存在的差异强烈暗示需要超越标准 $Λ$CDM 宇宙学的新物理机制，特别是那些影响宇宙晚期膨胀历史的机制。

Abstract: The $Λ$CDM cosmological model has been successful in explaining many astronomical observations. However, recent observations increasingly point to deviations from the standard $Λ$CDM framework. Among these, one of the most significant discrepancies is the \textit{Hubble tension}, which refers to the difference in values obtained for the Hubble constant $H_0$ from high-redshift measurement and local observation. To address this issue, numerous cosmological models and methodological approaches have been proposed. This review offers a concise overview of recent progress in resolving the Hubble tension. The combination of Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (BAO) and uncalibrated Type Ia supernovae data yields a value for $H_0$ that is significantly higher than the $Λ$CDM predication based on early-universe probes, even without incorporating local distance ladder constraints. This result indicates that the origin of the Hubble tension lies in new physics at low redshifts. Our findings suggest that although many unresolved systematics persist in current observations, they are insufficient to account for the magnitude of the current Hubble tension. This implies the likely existence of new physical mechanisms that have yet to be discovered.

</details>

*Keith R. Dienes,Jessica N. Howard,Fei Huang,Yuan-Zhen Li,Brooks Thomas*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper explores using a 1D convolutional neural network (CNN) to reconstruct the primordial dark-matter phase-space distribution from the matter power spectrum, outperforming a previous empirical analytic formula in both accuracy and applicability to diverse power spectra.
\n\n
本文探讨使用一维卷积神经网络（CNN）从物质功率谱中重构原初暗物质相空间分布，相比先前的经验解析公式，在准确性和对多种功率谱的适用性方面均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to accurately infer the primordial dark-matter phase-space distribution $f(p)$—which encodes information about dark-matter production mechanisms in the early universe—from observable cosmological data like the matter power spectrum $P(k)$. Traditional analytic methods, such as an existing empirical formula, work well for many cases but may fail for non-thermal, multi-modal, or otherwise complex distributions. Machine learning offers a data-driven alternative to capture these intricate features more faithfully.
\n\n
动机源于需要从可观测宇宙学数据（如物质功率谱 $P(k)$）中精确推断原初暗物质相空间分布 $f(p)$——该分布编码了早期宇宙中暗物质产生机制的信息。传统的解析方法（如已有的经验公式）在许多情况下有效，但对于非热、多峰或其他复杂分布可能失效。机器学习提供了一种数据驱动的替代方案，能更忠实地捕捉这些复杂特征。

Method: The authors employ a one-dimensional convolutional neural network (1D CNN) trained on simulated pairs of matter power spectra $P(k)$ and corresponding primordial dark-matter phase-space distributions $f(p)$. The network learns the nonlinear mapping $P(k) \\mapsto f(p)$, bypassing assumptions inherent in the earlier empirical analytic approach. The training data includes scenarios with non-thermal and multi-modal $f(p)$ to ensure robustness and generalization.
\n\n
作者采用一维卷积神经网络（1D CNN），在模拟生成的物质功率谱 $P(k)$ 与其对应的原初暗物质相空间分布 $f(p)$ 的配对数据上进行训练。该网络学习非线性映射 $P(k) \\mapsto f(p)$，避免了早期经验解析方法中的假设。训练数据包含具有非热和多峰结构的 $f(p)$ 场景，以确保模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Result: The 1D CNN achieves higher reconstruction accuracy of $f(p)$ compared to the empirical formula, particularly for complex distributions (e.g., non-thermal or multi-modal). Moreover, it generalizes successfully to a broader class of input matter power spectra beyond those used in training, demonstrating superior flexibility and robustness.
\n\n
与经验公式相比，1D CNN 在重构 $f(p)$ 方面实现了更高的准确性，尤其对于复杂分布（如非热或多峰分布）。此外，它能成功泛化到比训练所用更广泛的输入物质功率谱类别，展现出更强的灵活性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Machine-learning techniques, specifically 1D CNNs, provide a powerful and flexible framework for inferring the primordial dark-matter phase-space distribution from the matter power spectrum, surpassing traditional analytic approximations in both precision and domain of applicability. This opens new avenues for probing dark-matter physics in the early universe using cosmological observables.
\n\n
机器学习技术，特别是1D CNN，为从物质功率谱推断原初暗物质相空间分布提供了一个强大而灵活的框架，在精度和适用范围上均超越了传统的解析近似方法。这为利用宇宙学观测量探索早期宇宙中的暗物质物理开辟了新途径。

Abstract: One effective way of learning about the production and properties of dark matter in the early universe is by extracting information about the primordial dark-matter phase-space distribution from the matter power spectrum. Several years ago a simple empirical formula was introduced which successfully reproduces most of the salient features of the primordial dark-matter phase-space distribution from the matter power spectrum -- even in situations in which this distribution is non-thermal, multi-modal, or exhibits other complicated features. Continuing this line of research, we investigate the extent to which machine-learning techniques can improve upon this analytic approach. Interestingly, we find that a one-dimensional convolutional neural network not only succeeds in reconstructing the dark-matter phase-space distribution with greater accuracy, but can also be applied to a broader range of matter power spectra.

### [82] [A local Universe catalogue of structures and voids dynamically identified using Cosmic-Flows4++ZOA peculiar velocities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13538)
*A. M. Hollinger,H. M. Courtois*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: This paper presents a new catalogue of 37 cosmic voids and 42 supercluster knots in the local Universe (z ≤ 0.1), identified using the V-web algorithm applied to the CF4++ZOA velocity field reconstruction, with robustness validated via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo ensembles.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cosmic voids and superclusters serve as complementary probes of gravitational dynamics and large-scale structure growth; constructing a robust, dynamically motivated catalogue of these structures in the nearby Universe enables more precise tests of cosmological models and gravity.

宇宙空洞和超星系团作为引力动力学和大尺度结构增长的互补探针；在近邻宇宙中构建一个鲁棒且基于动力学动机的此类结构目录，能够实现对宇宙学模型和引力理论更精确的检验。

Method: The authors apply the V-web algorithm—a dynamical classification method based on the eigenvalues of the velocity shear tensor—to the CF4++ZOA peculiar velocity catalogue out to $z=0.1$. They use Hamiltonian Monte Carlo realizations of the Bayesian-constrained reconstruction to assess uncertainties and exclude structures intersecting survey boundaries, yielding clean catalogues of voids and knots.
\n\n
作者将V-web算法——一种基于速度剪切张量特征值的动力学分类方法——应用于CF4++ZOA特殊速度目录（红移范围至$z=0.1$）。他们利用贝叶斯约束重构的Hamiltonian Monte Carlo系综评估不确定性，并剔除与巡天边界相交的结构，从而生成干净的空洞与结点目录。

Result: The final catalogues contain 37 voids with effective radii between 13 and 38 $h^{-1}$Mpc, and 42 knots with volumes ranging from $10^4$ to $3.3 \\times 10^5$ $h^{-3}$Mpc$^3$, all fully contained within the reconstructed survey volume and validated against reconstruction uncertainties.
\n\n
最终目录包含37个有效半径介于13至38 $h^{-1}$Mpc之间的空洞，以及42个体积范围从$10^4$到$3.3 \\times 10^5$ $h^{-3}$Mpc$^3$的结点，所有结构均完全包含在重构的巡天体积内，并通过重构不确定性验证。

Conclusion: This dynamically defined cosmic web catalogue provides a reliable observational basis for studying the evolution of large-scale structure, testing modified gravity, and calibrating simulations in the local Universe.
\n\n
这一基于动力学定义的宇宙网目录为研究大尺度结构演化、检验修改引力理论以及校准本地宇宙中的数值模拟提供了可靠的观测基础。

Abstract: Cosmic voids and superclusters are among the largest structures in the Universe and provide complementary probes of the growth of large-scale structure and the underlying gravitational field. We present a comprehensive catalogue of the main local Universe cosmic web voids and knots, using the updated CosmicFlows-4++ Zone of Avoidance (CF4++ZOA) catalogue out to redshift z=0.1. To do this we use the V-web algorithm which provides a dynamically motivated map of the the local cosmic web, tracing the major expanding and converging regions of the local Universe. The robustness of the catalogue is assessed using the ensemble of Hamiltonian Monte Carlo realizations of the CF4++ZOA reconstruction. We additionally remove any structure that exceeds the survey boundaries and present catalogues of 37 voids and 42 knot regions within the reconstructed survey volume. The identified emptying regions (voids) have effective radii ranging from 13 to 38 h-1Mpc. The high density converging regions (knots) have volumes ranging from 10^4 to 3.3x10^5 h-3 Mpc^3.

</details>
